### [1] [Hyper-LLaVA: Hyperbolic Uncertainty-aware Modality-Balanced Routing for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13742)
*Kunlun Xu,Yanqin Zhang,Wenwen Qiang,Jiahuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Hyper-LLaVA，通过双曲空间中的分布相似度建模与不确定性感知的模态自适应平衡来改进多模态持续指令微调中的参数路由，性能显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态持续指令微调（MCIT）的参数路由方法依赖样本到任务中心的相似度与等权跨模态融合，存在两个根本缺陷：(1) 模态内路由仅用样本到任务中心的距离，未充分利用任务内丰富的多样性信息，导致匹配次优；(2) 不同模态在各任务上的可靠性不同，存在任务间歧义的模态容易误导路由结果。

Method: 提出 Hyperbolic Uncertainty-aware Modality-Balanced Routing（Hyper-LLaVA），基于跨模态任务特征不确定性建模提升参数路由能力：(1) 模态内方面，在双曲空间（Hyperbolic space）中建模样本到任务分布的相似度，替代简单的样本到任务中心距离以改善任务匹配；(2) 模态间方面，量化每个模态内部的任务匹配歧义，实现跨模态任务匹配的自适应加权平衡，从而缓解不可靠模态带来的性能退化。

Result: 在模态内与模态间任务匹配的互补增强下，Hyper-LLaVA 以较大幅度超越现有最先进方法。源码已开源（https://github.com/zhoujiahuan1991/ICML2026-Hyper-LLaVA）。

Conclusion: 通过双曲空间分布相似度与不确定性驱动的模态平衡路由，可同时缓解模态内多样性利用不足与模态间可靠性差异两大问题，为多模态持续指令微调的参数路由提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT) aims to exploit the incrementally accumulated knowledge to process multimodal inputs of diverse tasks, where parameter routing plays an important role. State-of-the-art methods rely on sample-to-task center similarity and cross-modal fusion with equal weight during routing. However, such solutions face two fundamental flaws: (1) Within each modality, the sample-to-task center distance is sub-optimal for routing since the abundant intra-task diversity information is underleveraged. (2) Different modalities exhibit varying reliability across tasks, where the modality with inter-task ambiguity can easily misguide the routing result. To address these problems, we propose Hyperbolic Uncertainty-aware Modality-Balanced Routing (Hyper-LLaVA) to improve parameter routing capacity based on cross-modality task feature uncertainty modeling. Specifically, to improve intra-modality task matching, Hyper-LLaVA accesses the sample-to-task distribution similarity in the Hyperbolic space. Besides, to alleviate the degradation brought by unreliable modalities, Hyper-LLaVA quantifies the task matching ambiguity within each modality to achieve adaptive balancing between task matching across modalities. Based on the complementary intra- and inter-modality task matching enhancement, our Hyper-LLaVA outperforms state-of-the-art approaches by large margins. Our source code is available at https://github.com/zhoujiahuan1991/ICML2026-Hyper-LLaVA

</details>


### [2] [Circuit-MLLM: Topological Logic-Guided Latent-Space Visual Reasoning for Circuit Schematic Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15668)
*Jinyuan Deng,Yuqi Jiang,Wenjing Huang,Xin Li,Qi Sun,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Circuit-MLLM 多模态推理框架，将电路原理图拓扑分析重构为器件定位、路径追踪与潜空间序列推理，显著优于 GPT-5.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型虽在通用任务上表现优异，但电路原理图因元件布局密集、拓扑逻辑特殊，需要细粒度结构解析才能提取电气语义，现有模型难以胜任。

Method: 通过电路知识挖掘机制，将模型潜表征与多粒度电路视觉专家提取的结构化特征深度对齐，内化拓扑语义；并设计拓扑引导的序列化策略，使推理摆脱固定光栅扫描顺序，在潜空间中沿电路拓扑逻辑逐步推理。

Result: 在多种电路分析任务上稳定超越强基线，平均得分较 GPT-5.1 高出约 25%。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了以拓扑逻辑驱动潜空间序列推理在电路原理图分析中的有效性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Through pre-training on extensive text and image datasets, current multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance on general tasks. However, circuit schematics present a unique challenge for MLLMs due to their dense component layouts and distinct topological logic, demanding fine-grained structural parsing to extract the electrical semantics. To address this, we propose Circuit-MLLM, a multimodal reasoning framework that reformulates circuit topology analysis as a process of device localization, path tracing, and sequential reasoning within the latent space. We introduce a circuit knowledge mining mechanism that deeply aligns the model's latent representations with structurally rich features derived from multi-granularity circuit vision experts, enabling the model to effectively internalize topological semantics. Building upon these internalized semantics, we devise a topology-guided sequencing strategy that decouples reasoning from the rigid raster-scan order, enforcing stepwise inference along the circuit's topological logic in latent space. Across diverse circuit analysis tasks, Circuit-MLLM consistently outperforms strong baselines, notably achieving a 25% higher average score than GPT-5.1, which demonstrates the effectiveness of our framework in circuit schematic topology analysis. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/IC-Yuan/Circuit-MLLM.

</details>


### [3] [SyntheticDoc: A Large Synthetic Dataset for Document Unwarping and Illumination Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15503)
*Daniel Woortmann,Tanguy Magne,Olga Sorkine-Hornung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SyntheticDoc，一个含 100 万高分辨率程序化生成样本的大规模文档去扭曲与光照校正数据集，配备 UV、法线、反照率与阴影等像素级标注，用物理模拟器与路径追踪渲染，并在其上训练简单基线以验证其相对现有方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习文档矫正与光照校正的性能受训练数据制约；近十年社区主要依赖 Doc3D，但其规模与质量已日益受限，成为研究瓶颈。

Method: 构建 SyntheticDoc：100 万张高分辨率程序化生成训练样本，并含大规模验证与测试集；每样本配像素级精确的 UV 图、法线图、反照率与阴影标注；纸张几何由基于物理的模拟器生成，并用路径追踪渲染以保证物理准确性与照片真实感。

Result: 在 SyntheticDoc 上训练一个简单基线模型，并在文档去扭曲与光照校正两个任务上与当前最先进方法进行对比，报告其性能表现。

Conclusion: SyntheticDoc 旨在突破文档去扭曲的数据瓶颈；数据集与生成代码已公开（项目主页与 GitHub 链接），可支持后续研究。

Abstract: Deep learning models have become the standard tool for document rectification and illumination correction, yet their performance is fundamentally bound by their training data. For nearly a decade, the community has heavily relied on Doc3D, a pioneering but increasingly limited document unwarping dataset in terms of scale and quality. To address this bottleneck, we introduce SyntheticDoc, a massive, high-quality dataset designed to push the boundaries of document unwarping. SyntheticDoc is composed of 1,000,000 high-resolution procedurally generated training samples, alongside extensive validation and test sets. Each sample is paired with rich, pixel-perfect annotations, including UV maps, normal maps, albedo and shading. To ensure physical accuracy and photorealism, the paper geometries are generated via a physics-based simulator and rendered using a path tracer. To demonstrate the benefit of our dataset, we train a simple baseline model on SyntheticDoc and report on its performance in comparison to state-of-the-art methods on both document unwarping and illumination correction tasks. Our dataset is available at https://igl.ethz.ch/projects/SyntheticDoc/ and the code used to generate it at https://github.com/tanguymagne/SyntheticDoc .

</details>


### [4] [Pixel-wise Planarity for High-Precision Monocular Plane Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13246)
*Ahmetcan Yavuz,Alpay Ozkan,Rémi Pautrat,Shaohui Liu,Marc Pollefeys*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出像素级平面性预测框架，结合深度、法线与平面性置信度进行区域生长，实现稳健的单目平面分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目RGB图像平面分割受限于区域分组不精确与几何不一致的监督，易产生过分割和误检平面。

Method: 基于预训练单目几何骨干（预测深度与表面法线），引入专门planarity head估计每像素平面性置信度；推理时将深度、法线与平面性结合于轻量区域生长，强制几何一致性形成平面段。

Result: 分析现有真值标注，在严格距离阈值下存在显著几何不一致；在多个数据集上相比SOTA提升几何精度与分割质量，同时提高计算效率。

Conclusion: 像素级平面性预测与几何一致性区域生长有效提升单目平面分割的精度与效率，代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Plane segmentation from a single RGB image remains challenging due to imprecise region grouping and geometrically inconsistent supervision, often leading to over-segmentation and false planar detections. We propose instead a pixel-wise planarity prediction framework for robust monocular plane segmentation. Building on a pretrained monocular geometric backbone predicting depth and surface normals, we introduce a dedicated planarity head that estimates per-pixel planarity confidence. During inference, predicted depth, normals, and planarity are combined in a lightweight region-growing procedure that enforces geometric consistency when forming plane segments. We further analyze existing plane ground-truth annotations and demonstrate substantial geometric inconsistencies under strict distance thresholds. Across multiple datasets, our method achieves improved geometric precision and segmentation quality compared to prior state-of-the-art approaches, while improving computational efficiency. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/alpayozkan/PixelwisePlanarity.

</details>


### [5] [Mind2Cloud: EEG-to-Point Cloud Generation with Two-Granularity Diffusion Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13991)
*Yongyi Lu,Xiongfeng Huang,Zhijing Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Mind2Cloud，一个基于双粒度扩散解码的EEG到点云生成框架，通过时间感知解码器动态融合全局Transformer与局部PVCNN分支，在EEG-3D数据集12名被试上取得几何精度与语义对齐的领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从脑信号重建3D物体对理解人类视觉认知具有重要意义。现有EEG三维重建方法多采用统一的扩散解码器，忽略了EEG表征与扩散去噪过程本身在语义粒度上的演化特性，导致重建效果受限。

Method: Mind2Cloud采用双粒度扩散解码。核心是时间感知解码器，通过可学习融合掩码在扩散各时间步整合全局Transformer分支与局部Point-Voxel CNN（PVCNN）分支：在早期上采样阶段引入Transformer层以在高不确定性下捕捉全局物体结构，在后期阶段使用PVCNN模块精炼局部几何细节。该设计依据EEG视觉表征的层次性，随扩散去噪由粗到细的过程动态调整空间粒度。此外引入对抗精炼模块以提升几何真实感与语义一致性。

Result: 在EEG-3D数据集全部12名被试上的大量实验表明，Mind2Cloud在几何精度和语义对齐两方面均优于先前工作，为EEG到点云生成树立了新基准。

Conclusion: 双粒度、时间感知的扩散解码设计能有效契合EEG表征与去噪过程的层次演化，显著提升EEG到点云重建质量；源代码已开源。

Abstract: Reconstructing 3D objects from brain signals offers a promising avenue for understanding human visual cognition. While prior work has shown initial success using EEG signals for 3D reconstruction, existing methods typically employ a uniform diffusion decoder, overlooking the evolving semantic granularity of both EEG representations and the diffusion denoising process. In this paper, we propose Mind2Cloud, a novel EEG-to-point-cloud generation framework based on two-granularity diffusion decoding. The core of Mind2Cloud is a time-aware decoder that integrates a global Transformer branch and a local Point-Voxel CNN (PVCNN) branch across diffusion timesteps through a learnable fusion mask. Specifically, Transformer layers are incorporated into the early upsampling stages to capture global object structure under high uncertainty, while PVCNN modules are used in later stages to refine local geometric details. Inspired by the hierarchical nature of EEG-based visual representations, this design dynamically adapts its spatial granularity in accordance with the coarse-to-fine trajectory of diffusion denoising. We further introduce an adversarial refinement module to enhance geometric realism and semantic consistency. Extensive experiments on the EEG-3D dataset across all 12 subjects demonstrate that Mind2Cloud outperforms prior work in both geometric accuracy and semantic alignment, setting a new benchmark for EEG-to-point-cloud generation. Our source code is available at https://github.com/duasoi/Mind2Cloud.

</details>


### [6] [Unsupervised Point Cloud Registration via Training-Time Semantic Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15228)
*Kezheng Xiong,Shiyun Xu,Sheng Ao,Siqi Shen,Cheng Wang,Chenglu Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CAESAR：一种仅训练期使用现成3D分割模型引导的师生框架，通过双重线索引导重匹配、语义-几何标签挖掘与语义预测蒸馏，在无监督LiDAR点云配准中恢复并保持语义感知，KITTI与nuScenes上达SOTA，且零推理开销、无需语义标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LiDAR点云无监督配准受户外场景几何歧义影响，伪标签质量差、收敛次优，尤其对nuScenes等稀疏低分辨率扫描。研究发现配准模型隐含与配准精度强相关的语义感知，但该感知脆弱：无监督下的噪声监督会迅速侵蚀语义结构，导致性能崩溃。因此需在无监督设定下恢复并巩固这种语义感知。

Method: CAESAR采用师生框架，仅在训练期由现成3D分割模型引导。核心模块包括：(1) Dual-Cue Guided Re-Matching，观察到潜在内点匹配常被少量伪近邻掩埋于噪声特征空间，通过重选而非直接剔除恢复匹配；(2) train-only Semantic-Geometric Label Mining，进行轻量、批次特定的教师精炼，并在语义引导下挖掘可靠伪标签；(3) Semantic Predictive Distillation，在特征空间中巩固学生的语义感知。

Result: 在KITTI和nuScenes上取得最优性能（state-of-the-art），在极具挑战性的nuScenes基准上提升尤为显著。同时方法零推理开销，且配准数据无需任何语义标注。

Conclusion: CAESAR验证了无监督配准中利用隐式语义感知的可行性与有效性：通过训练期语义引导的伪标签挖掘与蒸馏，可显著提升配准精度而不增加推理成本，也无需语义标注，为稀疏LiDAR点云无监督配准提供了新思路。代码将公开。

Abstract: Unsupervised registration of large-scale LiDAR point clouds remains challenging due to the geometric ambiguity inherent in outdoor scenes, which degrades pseudo-label quality and leads to suboptimal convergence, particularly for sparse, low-resolution scans such as those from nuScenes. We reveal that registration models intrinsically encode semantic awareness that strongly correlates with registration accuracy, albeit without explicit semantic supervision. However, this native awareness is fragile: noisy supervision arising from geometric ambiguity in unsupervised settings rapidly erodes the learned semantic structure, causing performance collapse. To this end, we propose CAESAR, a teacher-student framework guided by an off-the-shelf 3D segmentation model exclusively during training. We observe that potential inlier matches are often buried just beneath a few spurious neighbors in the noisy feature space, motivating Dual-Cue Guided Re-Matching to recover them through reselection rather than simply rejecting. Building on this, a train-only Semantic-Geometric Label Mining performs lightweight, batch-specific teacher refinement and mines reliable pseudo-labels under semantic guidance. We further introduce Semantic Predictive Distillation to consolidate the student's semantic awareness in the feature space. Extensive experiments on KITTI and nuScenes demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, with pronounced gains on the challenging nuScenes benchmark. Crucially, CAESAR incurs zero inference overhead and requires no semantic annotations on the registration data. Code will be released.

</details>


### [7] [DNF-SR: Dual-Input and Negative-Aware Feature Fine-Tuning for Real-World Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15120)
*Shuhao Han,Wenjie Liao,Hayden Vance,Hang Dong,Rui Zhang,Chun-Le Guo,Chongyi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DNF-SR，一种双输入、负感知特征微调的单步扩散真实图像超分方法，兼顾保真度与感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单步扩散 Real-ISR 中，直接将低分辨率图像输入扩散模型会与模型原始输入分布产生差异；直接加噪虽可缩小分布差异，但会破坏 LR 图像内容。

Method: 采用双输入策略，将原始 LR 与加噪 LR 输入拼接后送入扩散图像编辑模型；利用加噪输入带来的随机性与多样性，提出训练后优化方法 NF2T，将多输出划分为正/负子集，并在图像与特征空间定义隐式策略改进方向。

Result: 大量实验表明 DNF-SR 优于其他方法。

Conclusion: DNF-SR 在保证高保真单步超分的同时提升了感知与内容一致性，NF2T 进一步增强了优化稳定性。

Abstract: Benefiting from the powerful generative priors of diffusion models, diffusion-based real-world image super-resolution (Real-ISR) methods have demonstrated impressive performance.To achieve efficient Real-ISR, several recent works have designed one-step diffusion-based models.Howerver, unmediatedly feeding LR into a diffusion model creates a distributional gap with the model's original input.A straightforward approach to reduce the distribution gap is to introduce noise to the LR latents. However, directly adding noise inevitably corrupts the content of the LR images.In this study, we propose DNF-SR, a Dual-input and Negative-aware Feature fine-tuning method for Real-ISR.Specifically, we use a dual-input strategy that concatenates the original LR image with the noisy LR input and feeds them into a diffusion-based image editing model, ensuring both high-fidelity one-step super-resolution and improved perceptual and content consistency.Additionally, the noise present in the noisy LR input introduces randomness and diversity into the outputs. We exploit this property and propose a post-training optimization method, Negative-aware Feature Fine-Tuning (NF2T), which guides the model toward producing higher-quality results.NF^2T classifies multiple outputs into positive and negative subsets and then defines implicit policy improvement directions in both the image and feature spaces, thereby further enhancing the stability of the optimization.Extensive experiments show that DNF-SR outperforms other methods.Code will be released.

</details>


### [8] [Integrating Multi-view Multi-light Surface Reconstruction into Cultural Heritage Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15833)
*Baptiste Brument,Robin Bruneau,Benjamin Coupry,Vincent Demoulin,Jean Mélou,Antoine Laurent,Fabien Castan,Jean-Denis Durou,Lilian Calvet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作将计算机视觉中的多视角、多光照表面重建方法集成到开源摄影测量框架Meshroom中，构建面向文化遗产的可用工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化遗产记录日益依赖基于图像的3D表面重建，摄影测量软件虽已使多视角工作流普及，但尚未常规利用更丰富的多视角、多光照数据（如RTI穹顶采集），无法充分发挥细尺度表面重建潜力。挑战在于将现有采集实践与前沿计算机视觉方法连接，并适配实际操作流程。

Method: 并非提出新算法，而是集成并暴露现有先进方法：完整的光度立体生态（标定、自标定与通用型）、自动物体掩膜，以及多视角法线-反射率积分，将其组装进Meshroom这一开源摄影测量框架，形成面向文化遗产的可用工作流。

Result: 在Meshroom中构建了一个中间软件层，位于计算机视觉研究代码与文化遗产实际应用之间，使近期技术更易使用与评估。

Conclusion: 该集成系统弥合了文化遗产采集实践与前沿计算机视觉方法之间的鸿沟，为从业者提供了可操作、可评估的细尺度表面重建工具，提升了多光照数据在遗产记录中的可用性。

Abstract: Cultural heritage documentation increasingly relies on image-based 3D surface reconstruction, with photogrammetry software making such workflows accessible to archaeologists, conservators, and heritage technicians. These tools have been successful for conventional multi-view acquisition, but they do not routinely exploit richer multi-view, multi-light data, despite its potential for improving fine-scale surface reconstruction. This limitation is particularly relevant in heritage contexts, where controlled-light acquisition devices such as RTI domes are already used to capture illumination-varying image sets. The challenge is therefore to connect these existing acquisition practices with recent computer vision methods in a form that can be used within operational heritage workflows. In this work, we address this need by integrating state-of-the-art components from computer vision for multi-view, multi-light surface reconstruction into Meshroom, an open-source photogrammetry framework. Rather than proposing a new reconstruction algorithm, our contribution is to assemble and expose existing advanced methods, namely a complete photometric stereo ecosystem (calibrated, self-calibrated and universal), automatic object masking, and multi-view normal-and-reflectance integration, within a usable heritage-oriented workflow. The proposed system thus provides an intermediate software layer between computer vision research code and practical cultural heritage applications, making recent techniques easier to use and evaluate.

</details>


### [9] [SJD-SV: Speculative Jacobi Decoding with Semantics Verification for Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13245)
*Baoquan Zhang,Bingqi Shan,Shihao Fang,Kenghong Lin,Xutao Li,Yunming Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SJD-SV方法，通过语义级验证替代逐token验证，解决了投机Jacobi解码中的token歧义问题，加速自回归图像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机Jacobi解码（SJD）在加速自回归图像生成时存在token歧义问题，其成因尚未得到合理解释，导致验证准确性受限。

Method: 对视觉token进行可视化分析，发现视觉token通常对应局部、细小、模糊的视觉细节，单一token难以准确表达语义；据此提出SJD-SV，利用token间的强纠错特性识别语义感知token子序列，并在语义子序列层面进行验证，而非逐token验证。该方法为插件式，可直接集成到现有SJD及其变体。

Result: 在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，现有SJD方法在集成SJD-SV后均获得显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: SJD-SV通过语义感知子序列验证有效缓解token歧义问题，作为一种即插即用模块，可普遍提升现有SJD方法的图像生成加速性能。

Abstract: Speculative Jacobi Decoding (SJD) is an important approach for accelerating autoregressive image generation. Although SJD has shown superior performance, recent studies point out that it usually suffers from a token ambiguity issue during token verification but its reason can not be well explained. To figure out this reason, in this paper, we conduct a visualization analysis on vision token and find that different from text tokens, vision tokens generally corresponds to some local, small, and unclear vision details, which means only using single token is difficult to accurately express a certain semantic, thereby causing token ambiguity issue. To this end, we propose a novel Speculative Jacobi Decoding with Semantics Verification (called SJD-SV), for accelerating autoregressive image generation. The key idea is that leveraging the strong correction characters between tokens to recognize semantic-aware token subsequence and then instead of perform token-by-token verification, turning to perform verification on semantic-aware token subsequence level for accelerating image generation. In particular, our method is plug-in, which can be directly integrated into existing SJD and its variants. Extensive experiments on various datasets show that existing SJD methods achieve significant performance improvement after integrating our SJD-SV method.

</details>


### [10] [Quantum-Gated LiteSSD: A Parameter-Efficient Lightweight Hybrid Quantum-Classical Framework for Forward-Looking Sonar Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14025)
*Niloy Kumar Mondal,Poulomi Sarker Puja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Quantum-Gated LiteSSD，一种参数量高效的混合量子-经典前视声呐目标检测器，将 QuCNet 式多电路量子处理重构为以恒等为中心的通道门控机制，用于空间特征调制，在参数量与精度间取得良好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前视声呐目标检测对水下感知至关重要，但嵌入式平台部署要求模型高度紧凑；因此需在极少参数下保持有效检测精度，作者探索量子计算以缓解该矛盾。

Method: 提出 Quantum-Gated LiteSSD 混合量子-经典检测器，将 QuCNet 风格的多电路量子处理改写为以恒等为中心的通道门控机制，实现对空间特征的调制；基于 LiteSSD 轻量架构，在 Marine Debris Watertank 与 UATD 前视声呐基准上评估。

Result: Watertank 上达到 90.84% mAP50，参数量约为 YOLO26s 的 1/62、SSD-VGG16 的 1/164.3；UATD 上以仅 0.150M 参数达到 70.37% mAP50，比 SSGA-YOLO 小约 4.1 倍，并保持有意义的多类别检测能力。

Conclusion: 量子门控机制可在极低参数量下实现有效的声呐目标检测，验证了混合量子-经典设计在嵌入式前视声呐感知中的参数-精度权衡优势。

Abstract: Forward-looking sonar object detection is essential for underwater perception, yet deployment on embedded platforms requires highly compact models. To address this challenge, we explore quantum computing and introduce Quantum-Gated LiteSSD, a parameter-efficient hybrid quantum--classical detector that reformulates QuCNet-style multi-circuit quantum processing as an identity-centered channel-gating mechanism for spatial feature modulation. Experiments on the Marine Debris Watertank dataset and UATD forward-looking sonar benchmarks demonstrate an effective parameter--accuracy trade-off. The proposed detector achieves 90.84% $\mathrm{mAP}_{50}$ on Watertank with approximately $62\times$ fewer parameters than YOLO26s and $164.3\times$ fewer parameters than SSD-VGG16. On UATD, the model achieves 70.37% $\mathrm{mAP}_{50}$ with only 0.150M parameters, making it approximately $4.1\times$ smaller than SSGA-YOLO while retaining meaningful multi-class detection capability.

</details>


### [11] [From Model Patterns to Abstract Semantics in Compositional Zero-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15649)
*Weize Li,Zhicheng Zhao,Fei Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CLEAR框架，将组合零样本学习中的基元变化视为上下文驱动的具体视觉线索激活，通过完形填空式推理与重排序提升未见组合识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组合零样本学习方法依赖视觉语言模型并对基元上下文变化进行显式建模，但受限于固定的变体容量以及抽象语义与具体语义之间的竞争，难以充分刻画基元的条件性变化。

Method: 提出CLEAR（CLoze-style rEAsoning-based Re-ranking）框架，受人类感知过程启发：以由粗到细的方式从基元候选集中提取条件变体，进行完形填空式推理以推断高层语义，并通过重排序校正对显著具体基元的预测偏差。

Result: 大量实验表明，CLEAR持续改进基础模型，并在具有挑战性的C-GQA和MIT-States数据集上超越当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 将基元变化重新理解为上下文驱动的具体视觉线索激活，并借助完形填空式推理与重排序，是提升组合零样本学习中未见组合识别效果的有效途径。

Abstract: Compositional Zero Shot Learning aims to recognize unseen compositions by recombining learned primitives. Recent methods rely on vision language models and attempt to explicitly model contextual variations of primitives through multiple representations. However, such approaches are limited by fixed variant capacity and competition between abstract and concrete semantics. In this work, we present a new perspective that views primitive variations as the context-driven activation of concrete visual cues rather than independent entities. Based on it, we propose CLEAR, a CLoze-style rEAsoning-based Re-ranking framework inspired by human perceptual processes. CLEAR extracts conditional variants from the primitive candidate set in a coarse-to-fine manner, performs cloze-style reasoning to infer high-level semantics, and re-ranks predictions to correct biases toward salient concrete primitives. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CLEAR consistently improves the Base Model and outperforms state-of-the-art methods on the challenging C-GQA and MIT-States datasets. Code is available at https://github.com/buptLwz/CLEAR.

</details>


### [12] [GEAR: From Dynamic Encoding to Dynamic Activation in Social Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13778)
*Jiaheng Chen,Jiaxing Li,Leixia Wang,Jianan Ju,Tinghe Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GEAR模型，在轨迹生成阶段动态激活个体运动与社会共振偏置项，实现对社会上下文的"生成感知"调控，在ETH-UCY、SDD、NBA上取得竞争性SOTA表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹预测方法多关注社会信息如何被编码，而忽视编码后的社会上下文在生成阶段如何生效。作者认为动态社会编码不等于动态社会激活：相同交互上下文在不同未来时间尺度与场景密度下需要不同的激活强度，交互证据强时增强、弱或嘈杂时抑制。

Method: 提出GEAR（Generation-aware bias Activation model for human trajectory prediction）。基于偏置分解的轨迹生成公式，在每个未来时间步、最终轨迹合成之前，动态激活个体运动偏置项与社会共振偏置项，从而显式控制个体与社会偏置分量参与生成的时间点与强度。

Result: 在ETH-UCY、SDD、NBA三个数据集上，GEAR稳定超越基于共振的基线方法，并达到有竞争力的SOTA性能；激活模式分析与密度分组误差分析验证了在生成阶段校准社会编码的重要性。

Conclusion: 动态社会编码并不必然带来动态社会激活；在轨迹生成阶段对编码的社会上下文进行显式校准与激活控制，是提升人机交互场景下轨迹预测性能的有效方向。

Abstract: Human trajectory prediction requires modeling both individual motion patterns and social interactions among agents. Existing methods have made substantial progress by using attention mechanisms, graph structures, and temporal encoders to capture dynamic social context. However, most of them primarily focus on how social information is encoded, while paying less explicit attention to how the encoded social context should take effect during future trajectory generation. In this paper, we argue that dynamic social encoding does not necessarily imply dynamic social activation. The same interaction context may require different activation strengths across future horizons and scene densities: social cues should be strengthened when interaction evidence is strong, but suppressed when they are weak or noisy. To address this issue, we propose GEAR, a generation-aware bias activation model for human trajectory prediction. Built upon a bias-decomposed trajectory generation formulation, GEAR dynamically activates the individual-motion and social-resonance bias terms at each future step before final trajectory composition. This allows the model to explicitly control when and how strongly individual and social bias components participate in generation. Experiments on ETH-UCY, SDD, and NBA show that GEAR consistently improves the resonance-based baseline and achieves competitive state-of-the-art performance. Further analyses of activation patterns and density-grouped errors validate the importance of calibrating encoded social context during trajectory generation. Our code is available at https://github.com/11isnotavailable/GEAR.git.

</details>


### [13] [SURE-Map: Self-Correcting Streaming Geometric Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15795)
*Mingkai Liu,Hao Zhao,Xingxing Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SURE-Map：自校正流式几何基础模型框架，通过跨视角几何不确定性与多时间尺度自校正，缓解累积误差与尺度漂移，在KITTI等长时基准上取得在线前馈重建的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式几何基础模型作为SLAM的替代方案，其逐帧预测受限于局部上下文，易受动态物体与弱纹理干扰；小规模局部误差会累积为严重几何畸变与长时程尺度漂移。因此可靠流式重建要求模型不仅能预测，还能自校正。

Method: 两大互补原则：1）显式建模跨视角几何不确定性——不同于仅反映单视角预测可靠性的深度/点置信度，该不确定性直接衡量联合预测的位姿与深度是否诱导出几何一致的跨视角像素对应关系；2）多时间尺度自校正——快速连续帧推理保持流式效率，稀疏关键帧窗口推理提供更长程几何证据，周期性重新校准近期轨迹的尺度，以消除缓慢累积的尺度误差。

Result: 在长时程基准上刷新在线前馈重建SOTA：KITTI上ATE-RMSE由24.00 m降至17.24 m，Oxford Spires由5.11 m降至4.74 m，VBR由31.37 m降至28.58 m；结合回环闭合优化后进一步降至15.17 m、4.63 m和22.12 m。

Conclusion: 自校正能力是流式几何基础模型应对误差累积与尺度漂移的关键；跨视角几何不确定性与多时间尺度校正的结合可显著提升长时程在线前馈重建精度，且与回环闭合等全局优化互补。

Abstract: Streaming geometric foundation models are emerging as a compelling alternative to SLAM systems. Yet this streaming nature introduces a fundamental issue: each prediction is made from limited context, which is vulnerable to dynamic objects and weak textures. Small local errors accumulate into severe geometric distortion and long-horizon scale drift. We argue that reliable streaming reconstruction requires geometric foundation models to be not only predictive, but also self-correcting. We introduce SURE-Map, a self-correcting framework built upon two complementary principles. First, we explicitly model cross-view geometric uncertainty. Unlike conventional depth or point confidence, which primarily reflects the reliability of individual-view prediction, our uncertainty directly measures whether the jointly predicted pose and depth induce geometrically consistent cross-view pixel correspondences. Second, because local correction alone cannot eliminate slowly accumulating scale errors, we introduce multi-timescale self-correction: fast consecutive-frame inference preserves streaming efficiency, while sparse keyframe-window inference provides longer-range geometric evidence to periodically recalibrate the scale of recent trajectories. SURE-Map establishes new state-of-the-art performance for online feed-forward reconstruction across long-horizon benchmarks, reducing ATE-RMSE from 24.00 to 17.24 m on KITTI, 5.11 to 4.74 m on Oxford Spires, and 31.37 to 28.58 m on VBR, with further improvements to 15.17, 4.63, and 22.12 m when incorporating loop-closure refinement. Project page: https://mingkai-liu.github.io/projects/sure-map/.

</details>


### [14] [ArtSociety: Multi-Agent Multimodal Collaboration for Art Emotion Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13240)
*Jian Li,Fanfan Ji,Jinxiang Lai,Ying Tai,Jian Yang,Xiao-Tong Yuan,Chengjie Wang,Yabiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArtSociety：多智能体框架，通过异构多模态专家+两个免训练控制器，联合预测艺术品细粒度情绪、效价/唤醒与属性描述，官方测试集Overall 0.8870。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AffectiveArt任务需联合预测细粒度情绪（12类，头尾比1549:1）、二元效价/唤醒及五项属性描述，子任务间存在强经验性权衡，单模型难以同时优化所有子任务。

Method: 构建异构多模态专家集：DINOv2-Giant视觉智能体（A1）、场景化CoT微调MLLM（A2）、三个闭源推理智能体（A3-A5）；配两个免训练控制器：(i) 稀有类感知投票仲裁器，为尾部情绪降低一致性阈值，利用跨智能体家族的误差去相关；(ii) 描述优先推理智能体，DESCRIBE-then-CLASSIFY思维链先强制视觉证据再定标签；任务路由策略将困难情绪任务交给五智能体集成，将近饱和的效价/唤醒与生成描述任务交给单一最强推理智能体。

Result: 官方测试集（1000件艺术品）Overall Score 0.8870（Classification 0.7789，Description 0.9952）。十一变体消融显示，当方法与规模饱和在约0.76后，决定性收益来自智能体协作与数据侧监督；30B MoE旧数据模型不优于8B好数据模型。

Conclusion: 多智能体协作与数据质量是性能提升的关键，而非模型规模；免训练协调控制器与任务路由可有效缓解子任务权衡。代码已开源。

Abstract: The AffectiveArt Multidimensional Art Emotion Understanding task asks to jointly predict an artwork's fine-grained emotion (12 classes, 1549:1 head-to-tail ratio), binary valence/arousal, and five attribute-grounded descriptions -- sub-tasks that exhibit strong empirical trade-offs, so the single-model solutions we tried do not jointly optimize all of them well. We present ArtSociety, a multi-agent framework that assembles heterogeneous multimodal experts -- a DINOv2-Giant vision agent (A1), a scene-grounded CoT fine-tuned MLLM (A2), and three closed-source reasoning agents (A3-A5) -- and coordinates them with two training-free controllers: (i) a rare-class-aware voting arbiter that lowers the agreement threshold for tail emotions, exploiting decorrelated error patterns across agent families; and (ii) a description-first reasoning agent whose DESCRIBE-then-CLASSIFY chain of thought forces visual evidence before label commitment, yielding near-perfect grounded descriptions. A task-routing policy directs the hard emotion task to the full five-agent ensemble while assigning the near-saturated valence/arousal and generative description tasks to the single strongest reasoning agent. On the official test set (1,000 artworks), ArtSociety achieves an Overall Score of 0.8870 (Classification 0.7789, Description 0.9952). An eleven-variant ablation study reveals that, once method and scale saturate at around 0.76, the decisive gains come from agent collaboration and data-side supervision -- a 30B MoE model trained on older data does not outperform an 8B model trained on better data. Code is available at https://github.com/swordlidev/ArtSociety

</details>


### [15] [SAM3D-Part: Interactive Part Selection and Generation from 3D Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15639)
*Jiahao Chang,Dong Du,Wanhu Sun,Yujian Zheng,Chuanyu Pan,Bowen Zhao,Chongjie Ye,Yuanming Hu,Xiaoguang Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SAM3D-Part 框架，从输入网格和部件提示出发，选择性生成完整可复用部件网格，并准确放回源坐标系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代三维资产创作常需按部件编辑、复用、动画化或制造，且用户往往只需若干特定部件而非完整分解。现有三维生成方法不论用户意图都会生成全部部件；可提示的三维分割方法通常只输出部分表面而非可复用的完整网格；图像条件式部件生成器在缺乏源网格直接条件时难以保留被遮挡几何与准确放置。

Method: SAM3D-Part：编码源网格为紧凑网格特征，并与渲染图像、选择性掩码、点图观测通过逐像素通道融合对齐；融合表征驱动前馈生成模型，仅输出被查询部件的完整网格；通过预测稠密逐体素对应，从分布式空间证据估计部件变换而非单一全局位姿编码；多部件顺序查询时，将已生成部件存入部件缓存并作为上下文约束复用。

Result: 大量实验与消融表明，SAM3D-Part 显著提升源对齐、降低条件成本，并实现一致的选择性部件生成，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: SAM3D-Part 以提示驱动方式实现选择性部件生成，兼顾完整网格输出、准确放置与多部件一致性，代码与权重将开源。

Abstract: Part-level control is essential for modern 3D asset creation, where objects are frequently edited, reused, animated, or fabricated through their individual components. In many such workflows, users need only several specific components rather than a complete object decomposition. However, existing 3D generation methods produce all parts regardless of user intent, while promptable 3D segmentation methods typically output partial surfaces instead of reusable complete meshes. In addition, image-conditioned part generators further struggle to preserve hidden geometry and accurate placement without directly conditioning on the source mesh. To address these problems, we present SAM3D-Part, a prompt-driven framework for selective part generation from input 3D object meshes. Given a source mesh and a part prompt, SAM3D-Part first encodes the source geometry into compact mesh features and aligns them with the rendered image, selective mask, and point-map observations via pixel-wise channel fusion. The fused representation conditions a feed-forward generative model to produce only the queried component as a completed mesh. To place the generated part back into the source coordinate frame, SAM3D-Part predicts dense per-voxel correspondences and estimates the part transformation from distributed spatial evidence rather than a single global pose code. For sequential multi-part queries, previously generated parts are stored in a part cache and reused as contextual constraints, reducing conflicts among independently requested components. Extensive experiments and ablations demonstrate that SAM3D-Part can significantly improve source alignment, reduce conditioning cost, and enable consistent selective part generation, achieving state-of-the-art. Code and weights will be available at https://github.com/Jiahao620/sam3d-part.

</details>


### [16] [Newton Deep Unfolding for Compressed Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14391)
*Changhua He,Xianchao Xiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个基于二阶优化的牛顿深度展开网络NDU-Net，用于压缩感知图像重建，在多种采样率下取得优异且鲁棒的恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩感知（CS）的深度展开方法多由一阶优化驱动，且未能充分利用重建过程中产生的优化状态信息，限制了重建性能。为突破这些局限，作者引入二阶优化思想。

Method: 提出牛顿深度展开网络NDU-Net。其包含两个核心模块：牛顿更新（NU）模块，用于估计牛顿型更新方向并生成刻画当前重建过程的优化状态；牛顿引导的多尺度先验（MP）模块，将上述优化状态融入多尺度特征恢复，使学到的先验能够自适应于当前重建阶段。

Result: 在不同压缩感知采样率下的实验结果表明，NDU-Net取得了有前景的重建性能，并展现出更强的鲁棒性。代码已开源于 https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/DNU-Net。

Conclusion: NDU-Net是首个将二阶优化用于CS重建的深度展开框架，验证了二阶优化与优化状态引导先验在提升重建质量与鲁棒性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Compressed sensing (CS) reconstructs images from highly limited measurements, but existing deep unfolding methods are typically driven by first-order optimization and weakly exploit the optimization states generated during reconstruction. To address these limitations, we propose a Newton deep unfolding network (NDU-Net), which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first deep unfolding framework that leverages second-order optimization for CS reconstruction. Specifically, NDU-Net introduces a Newton update (NU) module to estimate Newton-type update directions and generate optimization states that characterize the current reconstruction process. Furthermore, a Newton-guided multi-scale prior (MP) module is designed to incorporate these optimization states into multi-scale feature restoration, thereby enabling the learned prior to adapt to the current reconstruction stage. Experimental results under different CS ratios confirm that our proposed NDU-Net achieves promising reconstruction performance and exhibits enhanced robustness. Our code is available at https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/DNU-Net.

</details>


### [17] [Reconstructing Is Not Acting: Action-Centric Latent Dynamics Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15189)
*Dingjie Fu,Dianxing Shi,Yangyang Xu,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出潜在动作模型（LAMs）存在"重建-动作不匹配"问题，即更低的像素重建误差并不必然带来更好的潜在动态与下游表现，并提出以动作为中心、轻量化的ACT-LAM框架来解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LAMs通过从无标注视频中推断潜在动作并重建未来状态来学习动作表征。但作者发现重建误差与潜在动态质量、下游性能之间并不一致（reconstruction-action mismatch）。其根源在于重建式潜在动态建模的两个欠约束问题：(i) 逆向动力学模型（IDM）未被显式要求区分与动作相关的视觉变化和无关的外观干扰；(ii) 正向动力学模型（FDM）可利用当前状态的预测捷径，从而未能充分利用推断出的潜在动作。

Method: 提出ACT-LAM，一个以动作为中心的轻量化框架，同时强化动作提取与动作利用。其Action Query IDM（AQ-IDM）采用可学习的动作查询（action queries）与门控聚合，在不施加强信息瓶颈的前提下选择性提取丰富的动作相关转移动态线索；其Action Token FDM（AT-FDM）将潜在动作投影为action tokens，使其与不断演化的状态表征渐进式交互，实现持续的状态感知动作条件化。此外，框架精简了特征处理流程，将模型容量集中于潜在动态建模。

Result: 在多个机器人数据集与VP²基准上的大量实验表明，ACT-LAM在潜在动作一致性、正向动力学以及下游视觉规划性能上均更强，同时可训练参数更少、计算开销更低。在VP²聚合成功率上，较此前最优方法提升7.6%。

Conclusion: 通过显式强化动作提取（AQ-IDM）与动作利用（AT-FDM），ACT-LAM有效缓解了重建-动作不匹配问题，在性能与效率两方面均取得优势，并刷新了VP²基准的最优结果，验证了以动作为中心建模思路的有效性。

Abstract: Latent action models (LAMs) learn action representations from unlabeled videos by inferring latent actions from visual transitions and reconstructing future states. However, we identify a fundamental $\textbf{reconstruction-action mismatch}$: lower reconstruction error does not necessarily yield better latent dynamics or downstream performance. We attribute this mismatch to two underconstrained aspects of reconstruction-based latent dynamics modeling: (i) the inverse dynamics model (IDM) is not explicitly encouraged to distinguish action-related transitions from nuisance appearance, and (ii) the forward dynamics model (FDM) can underutilize the inferred latent action by exploiting predictive shortcuts from the current state. To address both limitations, we propose $\textbf{ACT-LAM}$, a lightweight action-centric framework that strengthens both action extraction and action utilization. Specifically, its Action Query IDM (AQ-IDM) employs learnable action queries and gated aggregation to selectively extract rich action-related transition cues without strong information bottlenecks. And its Action Token FDM (AT-FDM) projects latent actions into action tokens that progressively interact with evolving state representations, enabling continuous state-aware action conditioning. ACT-LAM further streamlines feature processing to concentrate model capacity on latent dynamics modeling. Extensive experiments on several robotic datasets and the VP$^2$ benchmark demonstrate stronger latent action consistency, forward dynamics, and downstream visual planning performance with fewer trainable parameters and lower computational overhead. In particular, ACT-LAM surpasses the previous state of the art by $\textbf{7.6%}$ on the aggregated VP$^2$ success rate. Codes at $\href{https://github.com/DingjieFu/ACT-LAM}{url}$.

</details>


### [18] [MCIQA-2K: A Multi-Dimensional Dataset and No-Reference Quality Assessment Benchmark for Colorized Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14495)
*Yunkai Zhuang,Qihang Yan,Zicheng Zhang,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向着色图像的无参考质量评估基准 MCIQA-2K 及配套多分支 NR-IQA 框架 MCIQA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像着色本质上是病态任务，同一灰度图可对应多种合理着色结果，因此传统全参考 IQA 指标无法准确反映人类对彩色化图像的感知偏好。

Method: 构建包含 2000 张由五种代表性着色模型生成的图像数据集，并提供三个感知维度（颜色晕染、语义颜色错位、全局自然性）的人工标注；在此基础上设计多分支无参考 IQA 框架 MCIQA。

Result: 实验表明 MCIQA 在所提基准上显著优于现有全参考与无参考 IQA 方法，并在多个常用 IQA 数据集上展现出有竞争力的泛化能力。

Conclusion: MCIQA-2K 与 MCIQA 为着色图像的无参考质量评估提供了有效基准与方法，数据集与代码已公开。

Abstract: Image colorization is an inherently ill-posed task, since a single grayscale image may correspond to multiple plausible colorized results. Consequently, conventional full-reference image quality assessment (IQA) metrics fail to accurately reflect human perceptual preferences for colorized images. In this paper, we present MCIQA-2K, a large-scale multi-dimensional benchmark specifically designed for no-reference quality assessment of colorized images. We construct a dataset containing 2,000 colorized images generated by five representative colorization models, together with human annotations across three perceptual dimensions: color smearing, semantic color misalignment, and global naturalness. Building upon the proposed benchmark, we further introduce MCIQA, a dedicated multi-branch NR-IQA framework for colorized images. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MCIQA significantly outperforms existing full-reference and no-reference IQA methods on the proposed benchmark, while also exhibiting competitive generalization capability on several widely-used IQA datasets. The dataset and code are publicly available at https://github.com/ARBEZ-ZEBRA/MCIQA.

</details>


### [19] [MorphoStyle: Motion Style Transfer with Morphology Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14189)
*Xin Feng,Eleonora D'Arnese,Mohan Sridharan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MorphoStyle框架，在形状条件FSQ-VAE上实现形状感知的动作风格迁移，通过对比风格编码、文本引导风格路由与流形保持风格调制解耦风格与内容，兼顾形状控制与风格迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作风格迁移方法默认标准体型，而形状感知生成器缺乏显式风格控制，二者割裂导致难以对非标准体型生成风格化动作；简单拼接风格迁移模块与形状感知生成器会出现风格参考中的动作内容泄漏，并破坏与形状一致的动力学特性。

Method: 基于形状条件有限标量量化变分自编码器（FSQ-VAE）构建框架，将风格迁移建模为模块化潜空间解耦：(i) 对比式风格编码器，提取与内容解耦的风格嵌入；(ii) 文本引导的风格路由机制，在文本-动作特征空间中定位风格相关关节；(iii) 流形保持风格调制器，以时间门控低秩偏移的形式将判别性风格嵌入注入内容特征。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，MorphoStyle在形状控制和动作风格迁移两方面均优于对比基线，同时提供可量化的形状控制能力。

Conclusion: 将风格与形状统一建模并借助潜空间解耦，可有效实现面向非标准体型的形状感知风格迁移，避免内容泄漏与动力学破坏。

Abstract: Human motion may be viewed as a combination of action content, style, and body morphology. Existing motion style transfer methods transfer a reference style onto a content motion while assuming a canonical body, whereas shape-aware motion generators adapt motion to a target shape without explicit style control. This separation of motion style and shape (morphology) makes it difficult to generate stylized motions for non-canonical bodies; naively combining a style transfer module with a shape-aware generator often leaks action content from the style reference and disrupts shape-consistent kinematics. We present MorphoStyle, a framework for shape-aware motion style transfer that is built on a shape-conditioned Finite-Scalar-Quantization Variational Auto-Encoder (FSQ-VAE). The key contribution is to pose the desired style transfer as modular latent disentanglement comprising: (i) a contrastive style encoder that extracts content-decoupled style embeddings; (ii) a text-guided style-routing mechanism that locates style-relevant joints in a text-motion feature space; and (iii) a manifold preserving style modulator that injects discriminative style embeddings in content features as a temporally-gated low-rank offset. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that MorphoStyle outperforms competing baselines in terms of both shape control and motion style transfer, while simultaneously providing quantitative shape control. For more details, please see project website: https://github.com/funkdub/MorphoStyle.

</details>


### [20] [Sampling headroom is not selection gain: a compute-value audit of test-time scaling for video world models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13257)
*Yuhua Jiang,Junjie Lu,Feifei Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Compute-Value Audit (CVA) 顺序框架，检验测试时缩放（TTS）中"采样空间扩大"能否真正转化为可部署的收益，并在视频世界模型上给出系统性负面结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTS 的收益取决于两点：额外算力能否生成更优候选，以及系统能否可靠地识别它们。在视频世界模型中，更宽的采样池可能包含更强 rollout，却无法改善最终被选中的输出——即"采样空间（sampling headroom）不等于选择收益（selection gain）"。作者希望区分这两者，并判断扩样带来的潜在机会能否在计入全部生成与验证成本后仍具部署价值。

Method: 提出 Compute-Value Audit (CVA) 顺序审计框架，分四个阶段：额外采样是否创造机会、可观测信号是否提供可靠状态、该状态是否支持有益动作、收益是否超过生成与验证的"全部入场费"（full entry fee）。在 192 个 Physics-IQ 场景上将候选池从 4 扩到 16，测试 Flow、Cycle、VideoReward 等选择器能否回收 oracle 提升；并跨三个生成器评估十二种自适应深度策略与均匀算力对比；另在 VideoPhy2 上用匹配 60-NFE 的 Predict-and-Perturb 干预做三次新种子复现；同时以 PRM800K 稀疏设定、MMLU-Pro 及特权配对未来（privileged paired future）作为对照。

Result: Physics-IQ 上池从 4 扩到 16 使 oracle 质量提升 +9.23 IQ（95% CI [+7.44, +11.14]），但 Flow、Cycle、VideoReward 均无法可靠回收该空间。跨三个生成器，十二种自适应深度策略无一优于均匀算力，仅回收 42–69% 的实测入场费。VideoPhy2 上匹配 60-NFE 的 Predict-and-Perturb 干预在三个新种子复现中均为负。对照中，anchor-explorer 在稀疏 PRM800K 设定通过全部四阶段，MMLU-Pro 暴露了预测状态与有用动作之间的差距，特权配对未来则给出视频上的正向上界。

Conclusion: 采样空间只有在能被转化为可靠决策、且其收益能承受全部算力开销时，才具有部署价值；视频世界模型当前的验证器与自适应算力策略尚不能实现这一转化，但该负面结论并非普遍适用。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) can improve generation only when additional compute produces better candidates and the system can reliably identify them. This distinction is especially important for video world models, where a wider sample pool may contain stronger rollouts without improving the output that is ultimately selected. We introduce the Compute-Value Audit (CVA), a sequential framework that asks whether extra sampling creates opportunity, observable signals provide a reliable state, that state supports a beneficial action, and the resulting gain exceeds the full entry fee of generation and verification. On 192 Physics-IQ scenes, expanding the pool from 4 to 16 candidates increases oracle quality by +9.23 IQ (95% CI [+7.44, +11.14]), but Flow, Cycle, and VideoReward fail to recover this headroom reliably. Across three generators, none of twelve adaptive-depth policies outperforms uniform compute; they recover only 42-69% of the measured entry fee. A matched-60-NFE Predict-and-Perturb intervention on VideoPhy2 is likewise negative across three fresh-seed replicas. These negative results are not universal: anchor-explorer passes all four stages in a sparse PRM800K setting, MMLU-Pro exposes the gap between predictive state and useful action, and a privileged paired future establishes a positive video upper bound. Together, these results show that sampling headroom has deployment value only when it can be converted into a reliable decision whose benefit survives the complete compute charge. Code is available at https://github.com/YuhuaJiang2002/sampling-headroom-is-not-selection-gain.

</details>


### [21] [G-ray: Ray-Level Relative Geometric Position Encoding in Multi-View Vision Transformers under Camera Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15018)
*Shuo Zhang,Xin Su,Wei Wang,Jun Liu,Xinrui Zeng,Yongsen Chen,Chenjie Wang,Guibo Zhu,Jinqiao Wang,Bin Luo,Liangpei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出G-ray射线级相对位置编码，用相机局部射线角参数化旋转相位，实现跨投影的投影不变位置一致性，在异构相机多视图Transformer中取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图视觉Transformer在相机异构（视场不同或投影模型不同）时，现有旋转相对位置编码依赖图像平面坐标，导致相对相位依赖投影，跨投影注意力的几何线索不一致。

Method: G-ray以相机局部射线角参数化旋转相位，使同一相机局部射线对在不同投影下产生相同相对相位，具备投影不变的位置一致性；可直接使用或与现有编码集成，保留互补几何线索且不增加可学习参数。在RoPE、GTA、RayRoPE三种宿主编码中验证。

Result: 在三个异构3D重建基准、50视图设置下，G-ray在全部六项平均指标上领先，相较MapAnything将平均pointmap相对误差降低45.8%（双方均提供标定）；仅用同构针孔图像训练的3D重建模型无需重训即可处理混合针孔与非针孔输入，并在同构针孔协议上保持竞争力；NVS中GTA与RayRoPE在视点与FOV联合变化下均有提升。

Conclusion: G-ray通过射线级相位参数化解决了跨投影相对位置编码的几何不一致问题，兼容多种宿主编码，在重建与NVS任务中提升显著，并具备无需重训的跨投影泛化能力。

Abstract: We study relative position encoding for multi-view vision Transformers under camera heterogeneity, including varying fields of view (FoVs) or projection models. Existing rotary relative position encodings commonly use image-plane positional coordinates, producing projection-dependent relative phases and inconsistent geometric cues for cross-projection attention. We introduce G-ray, a ray-level relative position encoding whose rotary phases are parameterized by camera-local ray angles. The same camera-local ray pair induces the same relative phase across projections, providing projection-invariant positional consistency. G-ray can be used directly or integrated with existing encodings, retaining complementary geometric cues without additional learned parameters. We validate G-ray in three host encodings, RoPE, GTA, and RayRoPE, across 3D reconstruction and novel-view synthesis (NVS). Across three heterogeneous 3D reconstruction benchmarks at 50 views, G-ray leads all six averaged metrics and reduces mean pointmap relative error by 45.8% over MapAnything, with calibration supplied to both. Trained exclusively on homogeneous pinhole images, the 3D reconstruction model handles mixed pinhole and non-pinhole inputs without retraining and remains competitive on homogeneous pinhole 3D reconstruction protocols. For NVS, GTA and RayRoPE improve with G-ray under joint viewpoint and FoV variation. The project's webpage is available at https://g-ray-project.github.io/.

</details>


### [22] [ESAFusion: LiDAR--4-D Radar Fusion via Local Geometric Complementation and Multiscale Adaptive Interaction for 3-D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14619)
*Gang Ma,Senjie Hu,Junjie Liu,Chao Wang,Hui Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ESAFusion证据感知与尺度自适应框架，实现LiDAR-4D雷达融合3D目标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR-4D雷达融合可结合精确空间几何与运动、反射率线索，但雷达观测稀疏、两模态空间采样不一致，且模态与特征尺度的相对重要性随空间区域变化，导致跨模态互补与自适应融合困难。

Method: ESAFusion包含三个模块：证据感知雷达选择（ERS）利用运动与观测质量证据抑制雷达杂波并保留前景置信度；柱级互补编码器（PCE）借助邻近LiDAR柱的局部几何支撑改善空间采样不匹配下的跨模态互补；尺度内与尺度间自适应融合（ISAF）在BEV空间自适应调整不同模态与特征尺度的贡献。

Result: 在VoD数据集上取得对比方法中最高mAP：全标注区域74.60%，行驶走廊88.89%；两区域Cyclist类AP最高；运行速度19.23 FPS；在VoD-Fog上验证了LiDAR观测渐进退化下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ESAFusion通过局部几何互补与多尺度自适应交互，有效解决雷达稀疏与跨模态采样不匹配问题，在精度与鲁棒性上优于对比方法，源代码将公开。

Abstract: LiDAR--4-D radar fusion combines accurate spatial geometry with motion and reflectivity cues from radar, offering a promising solution for 3-D object detection in complex driving environments. However, sparse radar observations and differences in spatial sampling between the two modalities complicate reliable cross-modal complementation. Moreover, the relative importance of modalities and feature scales varies across spatial regions, making adaptive fusion challenging. To address these challenges, we propose ESAFusion, an evidence-aware and scale-adaptive framework that combines local geometric complementation with multiscale adaptive interaction. Specifically, we introduce an Evidence-Aware Radar Selection (ERS) module to suppress radar clutter using motion and observation-quality evidence while retaining foreground confidence for subsequent fusion. Then, the Pillar-Level Complementary Encoder (PCE) improves cross-modal complementation under mismatched spatial sampling using local geometric support from neighboring LiDAR pillars. We further design an Intra- and Inter-Scale Adaptive Fusion (ISAF) module to adaptively adjust the contributions of different modalities and feature scales in bird's-eye-view (BEV) space. Extensive experiments on the View-of-Delft (VoD) dataset show that ESAFusion achieves the highest mean average precision (mAP) among the compared methods, reaching 74.60% in the Entire Annotated Area and 88.89% in the Driving Corridor. It also attains the highest average precision (AP) for Cyclist among these methods in both regions while running at 19.23 FPS. Evaluations on VoD-Fog further demonstrate robustness under progressively degraded LiDAR observations. The source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/SenJieHu549/ESAFusion.

</details>


### [23] [Learning Continuous Source Responses For Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14316)
*Manni Cui,Ruiqi Liu,Zijian Yu,Hao Tan,Zibo Wei,Zian Wang,Ziheng Qin,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Jun Lan,Shu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CuRe框架将AI生成图像检测从二分类重构为对真伪混合比例的连续回归，并选取紧凑的源响应子空间以抑制捷径线索，在十个基准上平均平衡准确率达89.7%，领先次优方法5.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器在域内表现良好，但鲁棒性与跨生成器泛化能力有限，常归因于对捷径线索的过拟合。多数方法仅试图抑制捷径学习，却仍以二分类为训练任务，未反思任务本身如何塑造所学表示。

Method: 提出CuRe（Continuous Source Responses）框架，从训练任务视角重新审视真实性检测：将骨干网络适配重构为对真图—生成图混合比例的回归，提供更细粒度监督，促使模型捕捉超越二分类端点分离的真实性相关变化；进一步选取紧凑的源响应子空间以抑制无关变异，并限制最终分类器接触潜在捷径线索。

Result: 在十个公开基准上平均平衡准确率达89.7%，超过次优方法5.2个百分点；实验显示在不同视觉骨干网络上具有一致的泛化增益，并对常见图像退化表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过将真实性检测重构为连续源响应回归并约束分类器的信息通道，CuRe有效缓解捷径学习，显著提升跨生成器泛化与鲁棒性，为AI生成图像检测提供了新的任务设计范式。

Abstract: Advances in image generation have made synthetic images increasingly difficult to distinguish from real photographs, raising concerns about the trustworthiness of visual media. Existing AI-generated image detectors often perform well on in-domain data, but their robustness and cross-generator generalization remain limited. These limitations are commonly attributed to overfitting to shortcut cues. Although many methods seek to suppress shortcut learning, most retain binary classification as the training task without reconsidering how the task itself shapes the learned representations. We introduce CuRe, a framework for learning Continuous Source Responses that revisits authenticity detection from the perspective of the training task. CuRe reformulates backbone adaptation as regression of real-generated mixing ratios, providing finer supervision that encourages the model to capture authenticity-related variation beyond binary endpoint separation. We further select a compact source-response subspace to suppress nuisance variation and limit the final classifier's access to potential shortcut cues. Across ten public benchmarks, CuRe achieves an average balanced accuracy of 89.7%, exceeding the second-best method by 5.2 percentage points. Further experiments demonstrate consistent generalization gains across visual backbones and strong robustness to common image degradations. Code is available at https://github.com/manic-cui/CuRe

</details>


### [24] [LPA-CWM: A Learned Physical Adjudicator for Motion Reasoning with Counterfactual World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14073)
*Kunwei Wu,Xiang Liu,Guocai Yao,Junming Chen,Zhikang Chen,Min Zhang,Pengwei Wang,Sen Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将反事实世界模型（CWM）中的响应聚合建模为候选可靠性学习问题，提出用轻量学习式物理裁决器（LPA）预测候选权重，并引入完整性感知运动对应（CMC）评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CWM 从预训练视频预测器中提取运动，方式是比较事实预测与干预预测。但不同目标帧掩码下生成的响应可靠性不同，而现有方法采用均匀聚合权重，将它们等量对待，导致运动提取质量受限。

Method: 提出 LPA-CWM：在稠密 MOVi-F 轨迹上训练 3.0M 参数的轻量 Learned Physical Adjudicator（LPA），LPA 在无序候选集上比较视觉上下文与响应结构以预测相对权重，同时冻结 CWM 预测器与干预生成器。加权后的响应经过窗口化定位与一次配对再评估以恢复运动。另提出 CMC 评测协议，以真值锚定方式联合衡量定位、轨迹完整性、可见性与连续性，将可见动态点上的缺失预测计为失败。

Result: 在 DAVIS 与 Kinetics 子集上，LPA-CWM 的 DCA_avg 相比 Uniform CWM 分别提升 60.0% 与 29.0%，并在 TAP-Vid First 设置下提升跟踪精度。项目页：https://LPA-CWM.github.io。

Conclusion: 通过将响应聚合转化为候选可靠性学习，并以轻量裁决器预测相对权重，可显著提升反事实世界模型的运动提取效果；同时 CMC 提供了更全面、真值锚定的评估方式。

Abstract: Counterfactual world models (CWM) extract motion from pretrained video predictors by comparing factual and intervened predictions. However, responses generated under different target-frame masks vary in reliability, while uniform aggregation weights them equally. We formulate response aggregation as candidate reliability learning and propose LPA-CWM with a lightweight Learned Physical Adjudicator (LPA). Trained on dense MOVi-F trajectories, the 3.0M-parameter LPA compares visual context and response structure across an unordered candidate set to predict relative weights, while the CWM predictor and intervention generator remain frozen. The weighted responses undergo windowed localization and one paired re-evaluation to recover motion. We also introduce Completeness-aware Motion Correspondence (CMC), a ground-truth-anchored evaluation protocol that jointly measures localization, trajectory completeness, visibility, and continuity, counting missing predictions as failures on visible dynamic points. On the evaluated DAVIS and Kinetics subsets, LPA-CWM improves $\mathrm{DCA}_{\mathrm{avg}}$ over Uniform CWM by 60.0\% and 29.0\%, respectively, and also improves tracking accuracy under TAP-Vid First. A quick overview is available at https://LPA-CWM.github.io.

</details>


### [25] [RAIN: Region-Aware Inversion Network for Semantic Watermark Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14856)
*Zilai Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究扩散模型语义水印的高效提取问题，提出一种轻量、无需提示词的一步式提取器，通过扩展流匹配与条件回归将端点恢复分解为图像锚点与噪声残差，避免多步扩散反演和重复的 U-Net 评估，计算开销低于 OSI 与 FARI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gaussian-Shading 语义水印提取传统上需要多步扩散反演才能恢复初始噪声，计算成本高。近期一步式方法虽已显著降低开销，但仍有进一步优化的空间，需要更高效且理论解释清晰的提取方案。

Method: 基于扩展流匹配（extended flow matching）与条件回归（conditional regression）。核心观察是：在高信噪比图像端点附近，恢复扩展流匹配首步输出所给出的有用噪声统计量，比重建完整逆轨迹简单得多，而 Gaussian Shading 只要求恢复的隐变量落在正确的水印判决区域内。据此提出轻量、无需提示词的提取器，将端点恢复分解为图像型锚点（image-like anchor）与噪声导向残差（noise-oriented residual），以提升 GPU 并行计算利用率。

Result: 该方法避免了迭代反演和对扩散规模 U-Net 的重复评估，形成高效的一步式提取流程；其噪声提取的计算开销低于 OSI 和 FARI 两种方法。

Conclusion: 通过扩展流匹配视角重新审视语义水印提取，可在高信噪比端点以一步方式恢复满足判决需求的噪声统计量，兼顾效率与理论可解释性，为语义水印的实用化提取提供了低成本方案。

Abstract: Semantic watermarks for diffusion models embed ownership information into the generative process while preserving perceptual quality, but Gaussian-Shading extraction conventionally requires multi-step diffusion inversion to recover the initial noise. Recent one-step methods show that this cost can be reduced substantially. We study this problem through extended flow matching and conditional regression. The key observation is that, near the high-SNR image endpoint, recovering a useful noise statistic given by the first-step output of the extended flow matching in the high-SNR regime is much simpler than reconstructing the full inverse trajectory, and Gaussian Shading only requires the recovered latent to remain in the correct watermark decision region. Based on this observation, we propose a lightweight, prompt-free extractor that decomposes endpoint recovery into an image-like anchor and a noise-oriented residual, which increases the capability of the model to utilize GPU parallel computation. The resulting method avoids iterative inversion and repeated evaluation of a diffusion-scale U-Net, providing an efficient one-step extraction pipeline with a concise theoretical interpretation. The computational cost of extracting noise is lower than that of both OSI and FARI. The github repo is there: https://github.com/TheLovesOfLadyPurple/RAIN-lightweight-NN-for-one-step-semantic-watermark-extraction

</details>


### [26] [Selective Tool Use for Agentic Change Visual Question Answering in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14523)
*Yakoub Bazi,Mohamad M. Al Rahhal,Mohamed A. Mekhtiche,Mansour Zuair*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出选择性工具调用框架，使单一VLM在Change VQA中可自主决定直接作答或调用确定性变化分析工具，并构建工具增强的CDVQA扩展数据集（8类问题、3类工具），用LoRA适配Qwen3.5-4B联合学习直接回答、工具调用与证据条件回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在Change VQA上虽有进展，但在需要显式变化统计、面积测量或空间信息的问题上不可靠，缺乏精确的定量与空间推理能力。

Method: 选择性工具使用框架：VLM在双时相语义图上选择直接作答或调用确定性变化分析工具，工具返回结构化观测，再由同一VLM生成最终答案；构建CDVQA的工具增强扩展，含8个问题族与转变化、空间、时序三类工具，工具调用监督与观测由原始语义标注自动导出；用LoRA适配Qwen3.5-4B联合学习直接回答、工具调用与证据条件回答。

Result: 在7,164个测试问题上，使用参考语义图时整体准确率由73.77%提升至88.79%，平均族准确率由69.11%提升至89.65%；使用自动预测语义图时为77.47%整体准确率和75.06%平均族准确率。

Conclusion: 面向问题的语义证据对Change VQA有显著收益，但最终性能受语义预测质量影响；代码与工具增强标注将公开。

Abstract: Change visual question answering (Change VQA) requires understanding semantic changes across bi-temporal remote sensing images. Although vision language models (VLMs) have shown promising performance on this task, they remain unreliable when answering questions that require explicit transition statistics, area measurements, or spatial information. To address this limitation, we propose a selective tool use framework in which a single VLM either answers directly or invokes a deterministic change analysis tool to obtain question specific evidence. Specifically, the selected tool operates on bi-temporal semantic maps and returns a structured observation, which the same VLM uses to generate its final answer. To support this framework, we construct a tool augmented extension of CDVQA covering eight question families and three tools for transition, spatial, and temporal analysis. Tool use supervision and observations are derived automatically from the original semantic annotations, without additional manual labeling. We then adapt Qwen3.5-4B using Low Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to jointly learn direct answering, tool invocation, and evidence conditioned answering. Experiments on 7,164 test questions show that selective tool use with reference semantic maps improves overall accuracy from 73.77% to 88.79% and average family accuracy from 69.11% to 89.65%. When the semantic maps are predicted automatically, the framework achieves 77.47% overall accuracy and 75.06% average family accuracy. These results demonstrate the benefit of question-specific semantic evidence for Change VQA, while highlighting the influence of semantic prediction quality on the resulting performance. Code and tool-augmented annotations will be made publicly available at https://github.com/yakoubbazi/ToolChangeVQA.

</details>


### [27] [A 25-$μ$s/inf Event-driven Graph Neural Network Processor with Spatiotemporal Caching and Spline Convolution for Ultra-low-latency AI at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15241)
*Adrian Kneip,Martin Lefebvre,Daniel Gehrig,Victoria Catalán Pastor,Davide Scaramuzza,Marian Verhelst,Charlotte Frenkel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个可扩展至640×480分辨率的EV-GNN加速器ETHEREAL，通过邻域并行样条卷积引擎与2D/3D分离存储层次解决稀疏-不规则访问问题，实现25.6μs延迟和1.7μJ/事件能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DVS相机以逐像素、μs级时间分辨率产生事件，需要区别于传统帧基视觉的算法-硬件协同设计；EV-GNN虽是有前景的算法方案，但其混合了稠密规则计算与稀疏不规则访存，给硬件带来新挑战。

Method: ETHEREAL采用邻域并行样条卷积（spline convolution）引擎，以及2D/3D分离的存储层次，并引入新颖的感兴趣区域（region-of-interest）时空缓存机制。

Result: 在先进工作负载上实现端到端推理延迟25.6μs，每事件能耗1.7μJ。

Conclusion: ETHEREAL是首个可扩展至640×480分辨率的EV-GNN加速器，验证了针对事件驱动图神经网络的算法-硬件协同设计路径的有效性。

Abstract: Dynamic-vision-sensor (DVS) cameras generate events on a per-pixel basis with a $μ$s-level temporal resolution, calling for new algorithm-hardware co-design approaches compared to standard frame-based vision. While event-driven graph neural networks (EV-GNNs) emerge as a promising algorithmic solution, they raise new HW challenges by mixing dense-regular compute operations and sparse-irregular memory accesses. We present ETHEREAL, the first EV-GNN accelerator that scales to 640$\times$480 resolutions, thanks to a neighbor-parallel spline convolution engine and a 2D/3D-split memory hierarchy with a novel region-of-interest spatiotemporal caching mechanism. Measurement results demonstrate end-to-end inference with 25.6$μ$s latency and 1.7$μ$J energy per event on state-of-the-art workloads

</details>


### [28] [Compositional SVG Generation via VLM-Driven Hierarchical Semantic Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14657)
*Sehwan Park,Taehoon Kim,Geonhee Han,Dohyun Kim,Seung Wook Kim,Paul Hongsuck Seo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个由视觉语言模型（VLM）驱动的智能体框架，用于生成具有语义组合结构的可编辑SVG，并引入Semantic SVG Benchmark与子组件指标以评估结构组合性与可编辑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SVG生成流程多产出扁平、无语义的路径集合，编辑单个物体需人工识别其组成路径，缺乏结构组合性与可编辑性。

Method: 通过自顶向下分解、视觉定位以及提示驱动的非模态遮挡恢复，递归地将视觉场景解析为语义与几何层级，保证每个组件的几何完整性。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的原生预测结构在分组质量与可编辑性上超过了现有扁平生成方法的上限，同时保持了最先进的视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 该工作在语义组合式SVG生成及结构化评估方面建立了新范式，验证了基于层级的结构化生成在可编辑性上的优势。

Abstract: While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) excel at visual reasoning, generating structured, editable Scalable Vector Graphics (SVG) remains a fundamental challenge. Existing pipelines predominantly yield flat, semantically agnostic collections of paths, where editing a single object requires manually identifying its constituent paths. To address this, we propose a VLM-driven agentic framework for semantic compositional SVG generation. Our pipeline recursively parses visual scenes into semantic and geometric hierarchies via top-down decomposition, visual grounding, and prompt-driven amodal occlusion recovery, ensuring each component is geometrically complete. Furthermore, we introduce the Semantic SVG Benchmark with human-annotated semantic groups and novel sub-component metrics (Semantic Recall/Precision, PERE) to explicitly evaluate structural compositionality and functional editability. Experiments show that our natively predicted structures surpass the upper bounds of existing flat-generation methods in both grouping quality and editability, while maintaining state-of-the-art visual fidelity.

</details>


### [29] [Context-Aware Mutual Learning for Blind Image Inpainting and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14439)
*Haoru Zhao,Yufeng Wang,Zhaorui Gu,Bing Zheng,Haiyong Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Context-Aware Mutual Learning (CAML)框架，将掩码估计与图像修复联合并双向互用上下文信息，在盲修复及去雪/去阴影/去水印任务上达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲图像修复需在掩码未知时恢复受损图像。现有两阶段方法（掩码估计→修复）多强调掩码估计对修复的单向作用，忽视二者互惠关系：掩码估计可为修复提供更多语境语义，修复可为掩码估计提供更多上下文细节（纹理、边缘）。

Method: 提出CAML框架，联合掩码估计与图像修复以互相利用上下文信息。包含两个学习器：Inpainting-Guided Context-Mutual (IGCM)，从图像修复中获取互补的上下文细节以辅助掩码估计；Estimation-Guided Context-Mutual (EGCM)，利用掩码估计的上下文语义增强图像修复的语义理解。

Result: 消融实验验证CAML有效性；大量实验表明CAML在盲图像修复以及去雪、去阴影、去水印等附加视觉任务上均达state-of-the-art。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模掩码估计与图像修复的双向互惠关系，CAML有效增强修复质量与对未知真实污染的泛化能力，验证了双向互学习范式的优越性。

Abstract: Blind image inpainting, aiming to recover contaminated images in the case of unknown masks, is a challenging task. Motivated by the perspective of human vision and knowledge, blind image inpainting can be decomposed into two stages: mask estimation and image inpainting based on the estimated mask. The two-stage idea exhibits evident advantages in enhancing inpainting quality and augmenting the generalization capability of unknown real-world contamination by explicitly employing the estimated mask for image inpainting compared to one-stage scheme. This two-stage idea has also been intuitively implemented. However, existing two-stage methods excessively emphasize the unilateral relationship of mask estimation to image inpainting, and may overlook the mutual relations between them. Specifically, mask estimation can provide more contextual semantics for image inpainting to strengthen the understanding of semantics, and image inpainting can offer more contextual details (e.g., textures and edges) for mask estimation to improve the learning of details. In this work, we propose a novel Context-Aware Mutual Learning (CAML) framework for blind image inpainting that joints mask estimation and image inpainting to mutually exploit contextual information. In the CAML framework, we design the Inpainting-Guided Context-Mutual (IGCM) learner to acquire the complementary contextual details from image inpainting for assisting mask estimation, and the Estimation-Guided Context-Mutual (EGCM) learner to strengthen the understanding of contextual semantics from mask estimation for assisting image inpainting. Ablation studies validate the efficacy of our CAML. Extensive experiments show that our CAML achieves state-of-the-art performance on both blind image inpainting and additional vision tasks, i.e., snow removal, shadow removal, and watermark removal, indicating its superiority.

</details>


### [30] [GRAVA: Grounded Reasoning-to-Action Representation and Learning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15169)
*Xiao Liu,Haoyu Li,Jianghao Leng,Lin Wang,Chao Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRAVA 提出 Grounded Reasoning-to-Action（GRA）框架，将视觉接地、推理与动作生成统一到单一自回归流中，在 NAVSIM 基准上取得纯自回归驾驶模型的最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶 VLA 模型虽能"先推理后行动"，但其中间推理往往缺乏物理场景证据支撑，且与可执行行为关联松散，导致推理与驾驶行为脱节。

Method: GRA 将动作相关语言指代关联到 2D 视觉区域与自车物理状态，以轨迹锚定的类型化图组织物体交互与决策，并序列化为接地推理；单一 VLM 生成该推理及紧凑的 Executable Planner 动作，确定性地解码为连续轨迹。配套构建 agentic GRA 数据流水线（前向场景接地+后向轨迹锚定），产出 GR-NavSim（2.2M 接地问答对、70K GRA 推理轨迹）。采用渐进式训练：预训练建立接地认知，模仿学习建立推理-动作接口，强化学习与探索优化驾驶行为。

Result: 仅使用约 60% 可用人类驾驶示范作为动作监督，GRAVA-8B 在完整 NAVSIM 基准上达到纯自回归驾驶模型的 SOTA。内部长尾基准上，完整 GRA 相较仅动作预测将关键物体合规性与闭环驾驶得分分别提升 19.3% 和 20.5%。

Conclusion: 保留从接地推理到可执行动作生成过程中动作相关的物理证据，能显著提升驾驶模型性能，验证了 GRA 框架的有效性。

Abstract: Driving vision-language-action (VLA) models increasingly reason before acting, but their intermediate reasoning is often weakly grounded in physical scene evidence and loosely connected to executable behavior. We present GRAVA, a framework built around Grounded Reasoning-to-Action (GRA), which unifies grounding, reasoning, and action generation in a single autoregressive stream. GRA links action-relevant language references to 2D visual regions and ego-centric physical states, organizes object interactions and decisions in a trajectory-anchored typed graph, and serializes this structure into grounded reasoning. A single VLM generates this reasoning followed by a compact Executable Planner action that is deterministically decoded into a continuous trajectory. We further introduce an agentic GRA data construction pipeline that combines forward scene grounding with backward trajectory anchoring, and use it to build GR-NavSim with 2.2M grounded question-answer pairs and 70K GRA reasoning traces. A progressive training strategy develops grounded cognition through pre-training, establishes the reasoning-to-action interface through imitation, and improves driving behavior through reinforcement learning and exploration. Using about 60% of the available human driving demonstrations for action supervision, GRAVA-8B achieves state-of-the-art performance among purely autoregressive driving models on the full NAVSIM benchmark. On an internal long-tail benchmark, full GRA improves key-object compliance and Closed-loop Driving Score by 19.3% and 20.5% over action-only prediction, respectively. These results show the benefit of preserving action-relevant physical evidence from grounded reasoning through executable action generation.

</details>


### [31] [Lightweight Generalized DeepFake Face Detection with WAVIE: Wavelet Augmented Vision Intermediate Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14437)
*Arya Pulkit,Aditya Ruhela,Akarshan Kapoor,Arnav Bhavsar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WAVIE，一种在冻结CLIP骨干上融合空间与频率线索的端到端深度伪造检测架构，仅在FaceForensics++上训练即可在多个未见数据集上取得优越的跨域泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器在训练所用伪造方法上表现良好，但部署到未见过的篡改方法时准确率急剧下降，泛化能力不足，严重制约其在真实多媒体环境中的可靠性，给虚假信息治理、数字取证和以人为中心的AI系统带来挑战。

Method: WAVIE在冻结的CLIP骨干之上结合互补的空间与频率线索：通过轻量可学习模块投影中间Transformer嵌入，施加三级Daubechies-6（db6）离散小波变换（DWT），对低频分支进行细化并保留高频分支，再经逆DWT重建特征并完成分类。

Result: 仅在FaceForensics++上训练，WAVIE在帧级别取得AUROC：Celeb-DF-v1为0.852、Celeb-DF-v2为0.852、WildDeepFake（WDF）为0.831，优于多个最先进的泛化基线。消融实验证实小波模块与中间特征聚合对跨数据集性能均不可或缺。

Conclusion: 联合利用空间与频率域是提升跨数据集泛化的关键，WAVIE可作为真实场景深度伪造检测的强基线。

Abstract: Deepfake detection systems often exhibit significant performance degradation when deployed on unseen manipulation methods, limiting their reliability in real-world multimedia environments. This lack of generalization poses critical challenges for misinformation mitigation, digital forensics, and human-centric AI systems. Existing detectors perform well on the forgery methods they are trained on, but their accuracy drops sharply on unseen pipelines. To bridge this generalization gap, we propose WAVIE (Wavelet Augmented Vision Intermediate Embeddings), an end-to-end architecture that combines complementary spatial and frequency cues on top of a frozen CLIP backbone. WAVIE projects intermediate transformer embeddings through a lightweight learnable module, applies a three-level Daubechies-6 (db6) discrete wavelet transform (DWT), refines the low-frequency branch while preserving the high-frequency branch, reconstructs the feature via inverse DWT, and performs classification.
  Trained only on FaceForensics++, WAVIE achieves AUROC = 0.852 on Celeb-DF-v1, 0.852 on Celeb-DF-v2 and 0.831 on WildDeepFake (WDF) at the frame level, outperforming several state-of-the-art generalization baselines. Extensive ablation studies confirm the importance of both the wavelet module and the intermediate-feature aggregation for cross-dataset performance, highlighting the necessity of jointly leveraging spatial and frequency domains. These results position WAVIE as a strong baseline for deepfake detection in the wild.

</details>


### [32] [LLaDA-UI: Bringing Block-wise Diffusion to Vision-Language GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13287)
*Zhangxuan Gu,Haoxing Chen,Qi Qin,Yi Xin,Kai Gan,Lin Liu,Long Cui,Xiaomei Wang,Beitong Zhou,Yunzhu Zhang,Zhengwen Zeng,Changlong Gao,Weizhi Chen,Rongchao Zhang,Haoyuan Wu,Shuheng Shen,Changhua Meng,Weiqiang Wang,Jianguo Li,Zhenzhong Lan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LLaDA-UI，一个基于MoE的167亿参数块级扩散视觉语言GUI智能体，采用两阶段训练，在多平台GUI基准上显著优于Qwen2.5-VL-7B，并在六项基准中的四项超越Qwen3-VL-8B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLM）通过块并行、任意顺序生成实现高解码效率，适合延迟敏感场景；GUI智能体需实时感知屏幕并输出结构化、空间定位动作，是理想测试平台。但dLLM能否在保持并行解码优势的同时扩展为多模态GUI智能体尚不明确。

Method: LLaDA-UI为16.7B参数的MoE架构、块级扩散视觉语言GUI智能体。采用两阶段训练：先用通用多模态预训练对齐原生分辨率视觉编码器与LLaDA2.0-mini-base扩散语言骨干，再在移动、桌面、Web及grounding等多源数据上进行GUI智能体监督微调。

Result: 在多个平台的grounding与navigation基准上，LLaDA-UI显著优于Qwen2.5-VL-7B，并在六项GUI基准中的四项超过Qwen3-VL-8B。

Conclusion: 结果证明块级扩散是构建多模态GUI智能体的可行生成范式。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) achieve high decoding efficiency through block-parallel, arbitrary-order generation, making them attractive for latency-sensitive applications. GUI agents represent a natural testbed for this paradigm, as they must repeatedly perceive screen states and emit structured, spatially grounded actions in real time. However, whether dLLMs can be extended into capable multimodal GUI agents while preserving their parallel decoding advantage remains an open question. We present LLaDA-UI, a 16.7B-parameter MoE-based, block-wise diffusion vision-language GUI agent. LLaDA-UI follows a two-stage training pipeline: general multimodal pre-training aligns a native-resolution vision encoder with the LLaDA2.0-mini-base diffusion language backbone, followed by GUI-agent supervised fine-tuning on diverse mobile, desktop, web, and grounding data. Across widely adopted grounding benchmarks and navigation benchmarks spanning multiple platforms, LLaDA-UI substantially outperforms Qwen2.5-VL-7B and surpasses Qwen3-VL-8B on four of six reported GUI benchmarks. These results establish block-wise diffusion as a practical generative paradigm for multimodal GUI agents.

</details>


### [33] [Long-to-Short Video Evidence Reasoning for Grounded Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15224)
*Kaiyan Chen,Junbin Xiao,Xun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LOVER是一个用于grounded question answering（GQA）的"长到短视频证据"强化学习模型，通过课程学习、GQA奖励设计（IoP优于IoU）和自适应时间戳渲染三项创新，显著提升QA、grounding与grounded QA性能，并在NExT-GQA和ReXTime上取得开源模型SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的视频推理模型在grounded question answering任务上仍有提升空间：缺乏对证据时长的有效组织、'奖励设计对证据定位不够贴合、以及时间信息在帧上呈现不足，限制了QA、grounding与grounded QA的综合表现。

Method: 提出LOVER模型，包含三个模型无关且互补的设计：(1) Long-to-short Video Evidence Curriculum Learning，按证据时长组织RL训练，从长程grounding逐步过渡到短时推理；(2) GQA Rewards，采用IoP奖励替代IoU以更好地进行证据定位，而非严格时序跨度重叠；(3) Adaptive Timestamp Rendering，基于背景感知的位置与颜色选择自适应渲染时间戳到视频帧上，增强时序可观测性。

Result: 三个设计有效提升不同backbone上的QA、grounding与grounded QA性能；基于Time-R1构建的LOVER在NExT-GQA和ReXTime等主流GQA基准上取得开源模型中的新SOTA；消融实验验证了三个创新组件的有效性。

Conclusion: LOVER通过课程学习、IoP奖励与自适应时间戳渲染的协同设计，显著改善视频grounded QA表现，并验证了其模型无关性与组件互补性，为RL-based视频推理提供了有效方案。

Abstract: We present LOVER, a \underline{L}ong to sh\underline{O}rt \underline{V}ideo \underline{E}vidence \underline{R}einforced model for grounded question answering (GQA). LOVER highlights three innovations over existing reinforcement-learning (RL) based video reasoning models: (1) \textbf{Long-to-short Video Evidence Curriculum Learning}, which organizes RL training according to evidence duration and progressively adapts the model from long-range grounding to short-term reasoning; (2) \textbf{GQA Rewards}, which underscore the benefit of IoP reward over IoU for evidence spotting rather than strict temporal span overlap; (3) \textbf{Adaptive Timestamp Rendering}, which adaptively renders timestamps onto video frames using background-aware position and color selection to enhance temporal observability. The three designs are model-agnostic and reciprocal. They effectively improve QA, grounding, and grounded QA performance over different backbones. Notably, LOVER built on Time-R1 achieves new state-of-the-art (SOTA) results among open-source models on popular GQA benchmarks: NExT-GQA and ReXTime. Comprehensive ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of our three innovative components.

</details>


### [34] [Multimodal Foundation Models Adaptation based on Domain-Aware Relaxed Orthogonal Subspace for Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13654)
*Han Luo,Ruoyu Yang,Yinhe Liu,Yanfei Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DROS框架，将LoRA式低秩适配重构为数据驱动的子空间学习与柔性几何变换，缓解遥感任务中的子空间失配问题，并在多模态下扩展为MM-DROS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练基础模型微调成本高，PEFT（如LoRA）虽提升效率，但其固定低秩子空间未观测下游激活分布，在域偏移显著的遥感任务中坐标系统不佳，即"子空间失配"。

Method: 将权值分解条件化于下游训练分布估计的二阶激活统计量，使初始化反映遥感数据诱导的特征几何；再通过松弛正交参数化实现柔性几何变换；并扩展至多模态MM-DROS，跨模态共享变换结构以促进跨模态交互。

Result: 在多个遥感基准上达到SOTA，甚至超过全量微调，且无额外推理开销。

Conclusion: 以数据条件化子空间学习与柔性正交松弛为核心的DROS，有效缓解子空间失配，兼顾效率、多模态扩展与性能，优于全量微调。

Abstract: Pretrained foundation models (FMs) have achieved remarkable success in computer vision, yet their high fine-tuning cost limits practical deployment. Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods such as Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) improve efficiency by constraining updates to a predefined low-rank subspace. However, when applied to remote sensing tasks with substantial domain shifts, the fixed subspace is constructed without observing the downstream activation distribution and can therefore provide a poor coordinate system for adaptation, a phenomenon herein termed subspace mismatch. To address this issue, a unified framework is introduced, termed Domain-aware Relaxed Orthogonal Subspace adaptation (DROS), which reformulates low-rank adaptation as data-conditioned subspace learning and flexible subspace adaptation. Specifically, the weight decomposition is conditioned on second-order activation statistics estimated from the downstream training distribution, so that the initialization reflects the feature geometry actually induced by the remote-sensing data, followed by flexible geometric transformations enabled by a relaxed orthogonal parameterization. Furthermore, the framework is extended to multimodal settings (MM-DROS) by sharing transformation structures across modality-specific subspaces, facilitating efficient cross-modal interaction. Extensive experiments on multiple remote sensing benchmarks demonstrate that DROS achieves state-of-the-art performance, even surpassing full fine-tuning, without additional inference overhead.

</details>


### [35] [Multimodal-Multiresolution Foundation Model for Lunar Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13283)
*Paolo Fraccaro,Gabby Nyirjesy,Daniela Szwarcman,Himanshu Patil,Vishal Gaur,Rohit Lal,Rachel A. Slank,Geoffrey Dawson,Hiyam Debary,Michael K. Barker,Andrew Annex,Vishnu Viswanathan,Zachary Morse,Ethan I. Schaefer,Nikolaos Dionelis,Ankur Kumar,Campbell D. Watson,Manil Maskey,Rebekah I. Dawson-Rigas,Juan Bernabé-Moreno,Rahul Ramachandran,Sujit Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个月球遥感多模态基础模型，基于SomBench语料从头预训练，覆盖11种模态、两种空间分辨率，在地形生成、撞击坑检测、IMP分割和冰资源回归四类任务上匹配或超过ImageNet预训练基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球遥感领域缺乏专门的预训练基础模型；地球遥感或自然图像预训练模型（如ImageNet）对月球多模态、多分辨率数据的适配性有限，且缺乏统一的地理分区评测语料。

Method: 构建SomBench语料（近200万共配准瓦片束，11模态，1m与100m两尺度）；在TerraMind掩码token架构上做两项月球化扩展：将采集几何作为显式上下文输入，联合训练米级与百米级瓦片以共享权重；采用FlexiViT patch嵌入支持免重训练适配不同patch尺寸，模态化输入支持灵活多模态微调。

Result: 定性实验显示模型学到有意义的跨模态对应（由高程生成地形衍生量、由几何生成光照一致反射率）。四基准评测中（WAC/NAC尺度撞击坑检测、IMP分割、极区冰前景回归）预训练模型匹配或超越ImageNet基线及同架构随机初始化对照；多模态冰前景回归上预训练版本最佳，随机初始化也超过多数基线。WAC撞击坑检测上仅用50%数据即超越全量训练的ImageNet基线；LoRA在撞击坑检测与IMP分割上匹配或优于全量微调，冰回归则以全量微调最佳。

Conclusion: 预训练与架构均贡献增益；LoRA在多数任务中提供参数高效的替代方案，冰回归例外。作者开源预训练权重、基准数据集与微调代码，以推动可复现的月球AI研究。

Abstract: We present a multimodal foundation model for lunar remote sensing, pretrained from scratch on SomBench, a geographically partitioned corpus of nearly two million co-registered tile bundles spanning 11 modalities at two spatial scales (1 m/pixel and 100 m/pixel). The model adapts the TerraMind masked-token architecture with two lunar-specific extensions: acquisition geometry is provided as explicit context, and meter- and hundred-meter-scale tiles are trained jointly so that a single set of weights covers both resolutions. FlexiViT patch embeddings allow adaptation to different patch sizes without retraining, while modality-wise inputs enable flexible multimodal fine-tuning. Qualitative generation experiments suggest the model learns meaningful cross-modal correspondences, including terrain derivatives from elevation and illumination-consistent reflectance from geometry.
