We evaluate on four benchmarks: crater detection at WAC and NAC scales, irregular mare patch (IMP) segmentation, and polar ice prospectivity regression. Across tasks, the pretrained model matches or outperforms ImageNet-pretrained baselines and an architecturally identical random-init control. On multimodal ice prospectivity regression, pretrained variants achieve the best results, while the random-init model outperforms most baselines, suggesting gains arise from both the architecture and pretraining. Label efficiency is notable for WAC crater detection, where the pretrained model trained on 50% of the data exceeds the strongest ImageNet baseline trained on the full dataset. Among adaptation strategies, LoRA matches or surpasses full fine-tuning on crater detection and IMP segmentation while using far fewer trainable parameters, whereas full fine-tuning performs best for ice prospectivity regression. We release the pretrained checkpoint, benchmark datasets, and fine-tuning code to support reproducible lunar AI research.

</details>


### [36] [Open-UniMo: Towards Unified Motion-Language Understanding and Generation in the Open World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14615)
*Guocun Wang,Kenkun Liu,Guorui Song,Jing Lin,Zhe Huang,Luyuan Zhang,Dake Zhong,Choo Sin Wai,Xiaoguang Han,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出统一大型运动-语言模型 Open-UniMo，通过共享 token 空间、运动一致性 CoT 与两阶段训练，实现文本-运动生成与理解的双向统一，并发布 VLM 引导的基准 Open-MoBench，取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动-语言模型将运动视为语言模型的辅助模态，导致文本主导的表征与有限的跨模态交互；同时 next-token prediction 范式不适配长运动序列，自回归生成易累积预测误差。

Method: 基于 Qwen，将约 150K 文本词表扩展 64K 运动 token，使运动与语言共享统一 token 空间；引入运动一致性 Chain-of-Thought 作为连接语言语义与运动动力学的中间表征；采用两阶段训练：SFT 建立 CoT 引导的双向运动-语言映射，再用 GRPO 提升语义对齐并缓解自回归运动 token 生成的累积误差；在百万规模开放世界运动-语言数据上训练。

Result: 在传统指标与所提 Open-MoBench（覆盖 T2M、M2T 与双向一致性）上均达到 state-of-the-art；消融实验表明 M2T 理解并非主要受运动 token 词表规模限制，而与可学习的 T2M 生成路径耦合能获得更强的跨模态表征。

Conclusion: 生成可以促进理解：在基于自回归的运动-语言建模中，将 M2T 与 T2M 生成路径耦合能带来更强的跨模态表征，说明生成与理解应被统一建模而非分离。

Abstract: Unified motion generation and understanding is crucial for embodied AI systems that can both synthesize and interpret human actions in open-world environments. Existing motion-language models often treat motion as an auxiliary modality of a language model, leading to text-dominated representations and limited cross-modal interaction. Moreover, the next-token prediction paradigm is not naturally suited to long motion sequences, where autoregressive generation may accumulate prediction errors. To address these challenges, we propose Open-UniMo, a unified Large Motion-Language Model (LMLM) trained on million-scale open-world motion-language data. Open-UniMo promotes modality parity by extending Qwen's vocabulary of about 150K text tokens with 64K motion tokens, enabling motion and language to share a unified token space. We further introduce motion-consistent Chain-of-Thought reasoning as an intermediate representation to bridge language semantics and motion dynamics. Open-UniMo is trained with a two-stage pipeline, where supervised fine-tuning establishes CoT-guided bidirectional motion-language mapping and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) improves semantic alignment while mitigating cumulative errors in autoregressive motion-token generation. To support comprehensive evaluation, we propose Open-MoBench, a VLM-guided benchmark for assessing text-to-motion (T2M) generation, motion-to-text (M2T) understanding, and bidirectional consistency. Extensive experiments show that Open-UniMo achieves state-of-the-art performance on both conventional metrics and Open-MoBench. Furthermore, ablation studies reveal that M2T understanding is not primarily limited by motion-token vocabulary size; instead, coupling M2T with the learnable T2M generation path yields stronger cross-modal representations, demonstrating that generation can facilitate understanding in AR-based motion-language modeling.

</details>


### [37] [Pedestrian Crossing Intent Classification From Event-Based Vision Using Convolutional Spiking Neural Networks With Temporal Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13328)
*Henok Teklu,Mustafa Sakhai,Maciej Wielgosz,Matej Mertik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于动态视觉传感器（DVS）事件流的端到端行人过街意图预测流水线，训练卷积脉冲神经网络（Conv-SNN），在JAAD与CARLA数据集上取得高精度且低参数量、CPU可训练的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行人过街意图预测对自动驾驶安全至关重要，需在运动模糊、高动态范围、类别不平衡等苛刻条件下实时推理；传统帧式深度网络以固定帧率处理冗余RGB数据，限制时间分辨率与能效。

Method: （i）用v2e模拟器将JAAD真实驾驶视频转为合成DVS事件流；（ii）用CARLA模拟的DVS-PedX数据（正常与恶劣天气）增强训练；（iii）训练新型Conv-SNN，采用clip一致的DVS增强，二分类过街/不过街；细节包括LIF神经元动力学与替代梯度学习、类别平衡损失、JAAD 6倍过采样、70/15/15分层划分。

Result: JAAD DVS测试集95.83%准确率、F1=0.9695；正常CARLA DVS 97.79%准确率、F1=0.9478；恶劣天气CARLA DVS 94.78%准确率、F1=0.8369；模型仅1.07M参数，在CPU上训练。相比JAAD上先前帧式方法，精度追平或超越，且原生运行于稀疏时间表征。

Conclusion: 工作验证了基于事件流与脉冲神经网络的方案在行人过街意图预测上的有效性，兼具高精度、轻量化与能效优势；并分析了15个训练轮次的收敛行为、域迁移特性，并与代表性相关工作进行了定量比较。

Abstract: Anticipating whether a pedestrian will cross the road is safety-critical for autonomous vehicles, requiring real-time inference under challenging conditions including motion blur, high dynamic range, and class imbalance. Conventional frame-based deep networks process redundant RGB data at fixed frame rates, limiting their temporal resolution and energy efficiency. In this work we present an end-to-end pipeline that (i) converts real-world driving footage from the Joint Attention in Autonomous Driving (JAAD) dataset into synthetic dynamic vision sensor (DVS) event streams using the v2e simulator, (ii) augments training with the CARLA-simulated DVS sequences of the DVS-PedX dataset under both normal and adverse weather conditions, and (iii) trains a novel convolutional spiking neural network (Conv-SNN) with clip-consistent DVS augmentation to classify pedestrian crossing intent as binary: crossing or non-crossing. We detail all architectural decisions, the exact leaky-integrate-and-fire neuron dynamics with surrogate-gradient learning, the class-balanced loss formulation, JAAD oversampling at 6x, and a 70/15/15 stratified splitting protocol. The trained model achieves 95.83% accuracy and F1 = 0.9695 on the JAAD DVS test set, 97.79% accuracy and F1 = 0.9478 on normal CARLA DVS, and 94.78% accuracy and F1 = 0.8369 on adverse-weather CARLA DVS, all from a 1.07M-parameter architecture trained on CPU. Compared to prior frame-based approaches on JAAD, our method closes or surpasses the reported accuracy while operating natively on sparse temporal representations. We include a thorough analysis of the convergence behaviour across all 15 training epochs, domain transfer characteristics, and a quantitative comparison with representative related work.

</details>


### [38] [PhysBrain 1.5: From Vision-Language Models to Physical Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14973)
*DeepCybo Team,Yu Bin,Haipeng Cao,Zheng Chang,Kai Chen,Youning Chen,Kailin Deng,Yichao Du,Xiaotong Fu,Haoyang Ge,Yunlong Guo,Chenliu Hao,Jiyan He,Xuguo He,Yakun Hou,Kai Hu,Cong Huang,Tuopusen Huang,Yu Huang,Hong Li,Peize Li,Shijie Lian,Xiaopeng Lin,Yun Lin,Haibao Liu,Haochen Liu,Qiuzhi Liu,Shengcai Liu,Zhiqiang Liu,Tao Luo,Peng Ren,Shuo Ren,Chaoyi Ruan,Zhaolong Shen,Yukun Shi,Qiyuan Su,Yuxuan Tian,Yining Wang,Changti Wu,Hao Wu,Xueyin Xu,Ruoqi Yang,Zhaoyang Yang,Hang Yuan,Zhaoyang Zeng,Hanwen Zhang,Ruimeng Zhang,Yao Zhang,Yibo Zhang,Yuxiang Zhang,Zhirui Zhang,Ziyi Zhang,Zubin Zheng,Zishen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一模型 PhysBrain 1.5，将语言、动作与视觉预测编码为离散序列，用自回归方式联合训练，实现具身理解、动作生成与未来状态预测。8B 模型在 28 个具身理解基准上平均得分 72.5，达到开源最优，并在 14 个基准上领先，同时保留通用多模态能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受"观察—交互—环境变化"这一物理闭环启发，作者希望把物理环境理解、动作生成与未来状态预测三类能力统一到同一学习框架中，解决此前具身任务能力割裂的问题。

Method: 以通用视觉-语言模型为起点，将语言回复、末端执行器运动与稠密视觉目标编码为离散序列，并用自回归下一 token 预测联合优化。预训练阶段的具身监督全部来自人类交互视频，通过以任务为中心的片段，把语义与空间上下文与恢复的运动及后续观测配对；随后在人类演示、机器人轨迹与仿真经验的混合数据上进行监督微调。

Result: 在 28 个具身理解基准上，8B 模型平均得分 72.5，刷新开源最优，并与 GPT-6-Astra、Gemini 3.6 Flash 等领先闭源模型相当；在 14 个基准上取得最佳开源结果，同时保持通用多模态能力。定性示例显示模型可输出末端执行器轨迹，并预测空间对齐的 RGB、深度与机器人掩码等未来场景。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将理解、动作与预测统一为离散 token 序列进行自回归建模，是一条可行且有效的具身智能路径；仅用人类交互视频即可获得具身监督，并可通过混合数据微调迁移到机器人与仿真场景。

Abstract: We present PhysBrain 1.5, a unified model for understanding physical environments, generating actions, and predicting future states. Motivated by the physical loop of observation, interaction, and environmental change, we bring these capabilities into a common learning framework. Starting from a general vision--language model, we encode language responses, end-effector motion, and dense visual targets as discrete sequences and jointly optimize them with autoregressive next-token prediction. Pre-training draws its embodied supervision entirely from human interaction videos, using task-centered episodes to pair semantic and spatial context with recovered motion and subsequent observations. We then adapt the model through supervised fine-tuning on a mixture of human demonstrations, robot trajectories, and simulated experience. Across 28 embodied understanding benchmarks, our 8B model achieves an average score of 72.5, setting a new open-source state of the art and performing on par with leading proprietary models such as GPT-6-Astra and Gemini 3.6 Flash. It achieves the best open-source results on 14 benchmarks while retaining general multimodal capabilities. Beyond these understanding evaluations, qualitative examples show the model's ability to produce end-effector trajectories and predict future scenes through spatially aligned RGB, depth, and robot-mask outputs.

</details>


### [39] [Rethinking Camouflage Image Generation towards a Training-Free Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14377)
*Haodong Yang,Zhongling Huang,Gong Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeCam：免训练伪装图像生成框架，利用冻结的修复扩散模型与多模态先验，同时满足前景保持、语义兼容与外观同化，实现SOTA伪装效果并可作为检测数据合成监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有伪装图像生成（CIG）方法多依赖在伪装数据集上的任务特定训练，计算开销大，且泛化能力受训练域限制；同时CIG需同时满足前景保持、语义兼容与外观同化三项耦合要求，难以兼顾。

Method: 将CIG重新表述为免训练的"面向隐藏"范式：不更新参数，保留目标的同时降低其与合成背景的感知可分性，而非维持视觉显著性。以FreeCam实例化：基于冻结的修复扩散框架保持前景；Contextual Reasoning Module利用冻结多模态先验推断利于隐藏的环境以提升语义兼容；Intrinsic Appearance Module从前景提取低层颜色与纹理线索引导背景合成以实现外观同化。

Result: 大量实验表明，FreeCam无需任务特定训练即可达到SOTA的生成质量与伪装效果；生成图像可为伪装目标检测提供合成监督，并降低通用目标检测器下的目标可检测性。

Conclusion: 免训练、面向隐藏的CIG范式在无需参数更新与专用训练的前提下统一满足三项耦合要求，兼具生成质量、伪装有效性与跨域泛化能力，并具备作为检测训练数据与对抗通用检测器的应用价值。

Abstract: Camouflage image generation (CIG) aims to synthesize realistic camouflaged images by blending foreground objects into concealment-compatible background contexts. Achieving this objective requires jointly satisfying three coupled requirements: foreground preservation to retain target integrity, semantic compatibility to select plausible concealment contexts, and appearance assimilation to reduce visual discrepancies. Recent approaches predominantly rely on task-specific training on camouflage datasets to address these requirements, incurring substantial computational cost and limiting generalization beyond the training domain. To address these limitations, we formulate training-free CIG as a concealment-oriented paradigm that preserves the target while reducing its perceptual separability from the synthesized surroundings, rather than maintaining its visual prominence, without parameter updates. We instantiate this paradigm with FreeCam based on a frozen inpainting diffusion framework to preserve the foreground. Within this framework, a Contextual Reasoning Module exploits frozen multimodal priors to infer an environment favorable to concealment, thereby promoting semantic compatibility, while an Intrinsic Appearance Module extracts low-level color and texture cues from the foreground to guide background synthesis toward appearance assimilation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FreeCam achieves state-of-the-art generation quality and camouflage effectiveness without task-specific training, while its generated images provide synthetic supervision for camouflaged object detection and reduce target detectability under general object detectors.

</details>


### [40] [Robust Multi-Model Fitting through Learning Neighbor Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15348)
*Chang Nie,Guangming Wang,Zhe Liu,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LNR（Learning Neighbor Regions）——一种鲁棒的从粗到细多模型拟合框架，通过粗级模块预筛选优质最小集、细级模块编码邻域区域特征来细化并评分各假设，避免对采样与求解过程求导，在四类典型多模型拟合任务上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模型拟合是场景重建、混合现实等计算机视觉任务的基础，但现有方法受限于特征利用不充分、优化效率低、模型重叠以及流水线不可微等问题，性能提升受限。

Method: 提出粗到细框架LNR。粗级模块用神经网络提取并分析最小集的局部逐点关系与全局上下文几何特征，输出置信度以预筛选少量优质最小集，提升求解假设前的效率；细级模块为每个假设编码邻域区域特征（由邻近数据点的几何特征构成，可被多个区域共享），使网络能单独细化并评分每个假设以缓解模型重叠；训练直接基于数据点特征而非假设参数，从而避免对采样过程和模型求解器求导。

Result: 在四个经典多模型拟合任务上的大量实验表明，LNR达到当前最优（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: 分析表明LNR易于适配到各类鲁棒多模型拟合任务，具有良好的通用性与推广潜力。

Abstract: Multi-model fitting involves fitting multiple models accurately in a noisy environment. It is the basis for computer vision tasks such as scene reconstruction and mixed reality. However, its performance is often limited by insufficient feature utilization, inefficient optimization, model overlap, and the non-differentiable pipelines. To overcome these limitations, we introduce a robust coarse-to-fine framework called Learning Neighbor Regions (LNR). Recognizing that substantial computational resources are wasted on numerous bad minimum sets, we propose the coarse-level module. This module utilizes a neural network to extract and analyze geometric feature of both local point-wise relationships and global contextual information in minimum sets, outputting confidence to pre-select a small number of good minimum sets, thereby enhancing overall efficiency before solving hypotheses. To address model overlap, LNR encodes neighbor region features for each hypothesis in its fine-level module. These region features consist of geometric features of neighboring data points, which can be used by multiple regions simultaneously. This design allows the neural network to individually refine and score each hypothesis. Importantly, LNR is trained to learn directly from data point features rather than from the hypothesis parameters, thus avoiding differentiating the sampling process and the model solvers. Extensive experiments on four classic multi-model fitting tasks demonstrate that LNR achieves state-of-the-art performance. The analysis suggests that LNR can be easily adapted to various robust multi-model fitting tasks.

</details>


### [41] [SCOUT-SLAM: Structurally-Coupled Dual Uncertainty-Aware 3DGS SLAM in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14634)
*Kumaran Karthik,Pramat Shastri Jois,Suresh Sundaram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SCOUT-SLAM，一种结构耦合的双不确定性框架，同时估计跟踪不确定性与重建不确定性，缓解跟踪精度与重建质量之间的循环依赖，在动态数据集上实现SOTA跟踪精度与无伪影静态重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS-SLAM在快速运动和杂乱动态场景中，依赖底层场景重建的稳定性来建模不确定性，导致跟踪精度与重建质量形成循环依赖：重建不稳定会削弱不确定性建模，进而损害相机跟踪与静态场景重建。

Method: SCOUT-SLAM采用共享基础网络估计两种不确定性：通过在该网络上施加低秩适配（LoRA），并基于多视角特征一致性训练，得到不单纯依赖重建质量的跟踪不确定性；同时利用空间自适应先验调制网络训练目标，使重建不稳定不会抬高静态区域的不确定性，从而保持两支路共享表示的完整性。

Result: 在动态基准数据集（TUM RGB-D、Bonn Dynamic、Wild-SLAM MoCap）上的评估表明，SCOUT-SLAM取得了目前最优的相机跟踪精度，并实现无伪影的静态场景重建。

Conclusion: 通过结构耦合的双不确定性建模，SCOUT-SLAM有效打破跟踪与重建之间的循环依赖，为动态环境下的3DGS-SLAM提供了兼具鲁棒跟踪与高质量静态重建的解决方案。

Abstract: Recently, 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM (3DGS-SLAM) has gained significant momentum in simultaneous localization and 3DGS scene reconstruction. In real-world scenarios with rapid camera motion and cluttered dynamic environments, existing methods rely on the stability of the underlying scene reconstruction to model uncertainty. This leads to a circular dependency between camera tracking accuracy and reconstruction quality: reconstruction instabilities degrade uncertainty modeling, which affects accurate camera tracking and static scene reconstruction. To address this, the paper proposes SCOUT-SLAM, a structurally-coupled dual-uncertainty framework in which both uncertainties are estimated from a shared base network. A low-rank adaptation of this network, trained on multi-view feature consistency, estimates a tracking uncertainty that does not depend solely on the reconstruction quality. A spatially-adaptive prior modulates the network's training objective so that reconstruction instability does not inflate uncertainty on static regions, keeping the shared representation intact for both branches. Evaluations on dynamic benchmarks (TUM RGB-D, Bonn Dynamic, Wild-SLAM MoCap) demonstrate that SCOUT-SLAM achieves state-of-the-art camera tracking accuracy and artifact-free static scene reconstruction.

</details>


### [42] [Tele360: Real-Time Feed-Forward Human Reconstruction from Sparse Unposed Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15032)
*Hanzhang Tu,Zhanfeng Liao,Wei Min,Jiajun Zhang,Yebin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Tele360 是首个从稀疏、未标定 RGB 流进行动态人体重建与实时自由视点可视化的前馈系统，联合估计相机位姿并重建逐时刻动态 3D 高斯表示，在单张消费级 GPU 上实现 2K 输入到渲染超 25 FPS 的实时性能，并达到工作室基准上的最优视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沉浸式通信与交互式数字体验需要真实人体的实时自由视点可视化。现有方法存在两大瓶颈：一是依赖计算昂贵的逐场景优化，二是要求相机已标定且输入分辨率低，因此难以实现实时、高分辨率的实际部署。核心动机是在稀疏、未标定位姿的 RGB 输入下实现实时高分辨率动态人体重建与自由视点渲染。

Method: 1) 设计轻量级稀疏感知多视图 Transformer 骨干，对前景人体区域做 token 化，并通过共享场景 token 保留全局上下文；2) 采用全 Transformer 的高斯解码器，缓解卷积导致的过度平滑，同时保持稀疏高效解码；3) 引入混合特征金字塔，将多尺度外观线索注入几何预测；4) 提出轻量级可微 Levenberg-Marquardt 相机细化层，增强多视图一致性与几何对齐；5) 通过教师-学生蒸馏，从大型视觉-几何基础模型迁移多视图几何先验，以稳定稀疏、未标定输入下的学习；6) 将预测的高斯图通过视频编解码器流式传输到远端设备，用于交互式自由视点渲染。整个相机位姿估计与动态 3D 高斯重建在单次前向传播中联合完成。

Result: 大量实验表明，Tele360 在工作室基准上取得最先进的视觉质量，同时在单张消费级 GPU 上支持 2K 分辨率输入到渲染的实时运行，帧率超过 25 FPS。此外，在多相机设置下采集的额外序列显示了其在多种受试者、服装和运动下的良好性能。

Conclusion: Tele360 通过前馈式联合位姿估计与动态高斯重建、稀疏感知 Transformer 架构、相机细化层以及几何先验蒸馏，首次实现了从稀疏、未标定 RGB 流的实时自由视点人体可视化，在质量与效率上均超越现有方法，为沉浸式通信和交互式数字体验提供了实用的高分辨率实时方案。

Abstract: Live free-viewpoint visualization of real humans is critical for immersive communication and interactive digital experiences. Existing methods either rely on computationally expensive optimization or require calibrated cameras and low-resolution inputs, making real-time high-resolution deployment impractical. In this work, we present Tele360, the first real-time feed-forward system for dynamic human reconstruction and live free-viewpoint visualization from sparse, unposed RGB streams. Our system jointly estimates camera poses and reconstructs a dynamic 3D Gaussian representation for each time instance in a single forward pass. To achieve this, we start by designing a lightweight sparsity-aware multi-view transformer backbone that tokenizes foreground human regions while preserving global context through a shared scene token. We then employ a fully transformer-based Gaussian decoder to mitigate convolution-induced over-smoothing while keeping decoding sparse and efficient. In addition, we introduce a hybrid feature pyramid that injects multi-scale appearance cues into geometry prediction. We further introduce a lightweight differentiable Levenberg-Marquardt camera refinement layer to enhance multi-view consistency and geometric alignment. Moreover, to stabilize learning under sparse, unposed inputs, we transfer multi-view geometry priors from a large visual-geometry foundation model via teacher-student distillation. Finally, the predicted Gaussian maps are streamed with video codecs to remote devices for interactive free-viewpoint rendering. Extensive experiments show that Tele360 achieves state-of-the-art visual quality on studio benchmarks while supporting real-time 2K input-to-rendering at over 25 FPS on a single consumer GPU. Additional captured sequences illustrate its performance across varied subjects, clothing, and motions under our multi-camera setup.

</details>


### [43] [V-ICAL Bench: Evaluating Video In-Context Learning for Multimodal Agents in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15683)
*Ziqian Fan,Shibo Xu,Junjie Li,Xiangyu Zhao,Shengyuan Ding,Yifan Yang,Zhenjie Yang,Haodong Duan,Yue Zhou,Zhihang Zhong,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 V-ICAL 基准，用于评估多模态智能体基于视频演示的上下文学习（ICL）能力，覆盖 37 个环境、342 个交互任务，评测显示现有 19 个先进多模态智能体表现远低于人类水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态 ICL 研究不足，尤其缺乏交互环境中视频演示的评估手段；多模态智能体从视频中学习面临将演示转化为可执行策略、在新视觉状态下落地、并依环境反馈迭代修正动作等独特挑战，因此需要一个专门基准。

Method: 构建 V-ICAL 基准：包含 37 个环境中的 342 个交互任务，以人工整理的演示视频作为任务特定的行为范例；智能体从指定目标初始状态出发，通过持续交互接受评估；基准将上下文知识归纳与状态接地、时序记忆、规划、动态环境适应等核心智能体能力相衔接；并对 19 个先进多模态智能体进行广泛评测与受控对比。

Result: 表现最佳的 Seed-2.1-Pro 仅得 54.4/100，Gemini-3.1-Pro、GPT-5.6 等领先模型均未超过 50 分，远低于人类基线的 83.6；受控对比表明当前智能体难以可靠地将视频范例转化为有效策略，未能带来稳定的性能提升。

Conclusion: V-ICAL 揭示了多模态智能体在 ICL 能力上的关键缺口，凸显未来研究的迫切需求，为视频驱动的多模态智能体学习提供了标准化评测基础。

Abstract: While In-Context Learning (ICL) enables models to adapt from exemplars without parameter updates, multimodal ICL remains largely underexplored, particularly regarding video demonstrations in interactive environments. For multimodal agents, learning from videos presents unique challenges: they must translate in-context demonstrations into executable policies, ground these policies in novel visual states, and iteratively refine actions based on environmental feedback. We introduce V-ICAL, a novel benchmark designed to evaluate video-based ICL for multimodal agents. Comprising 342 interactive tasks across 37 environments, V-ICAL utilizes human-curated demonstration videos as task-specific behavioral exemplars, evaluating agents through sustained interaction from a target initialization. The benchmark seamlessly connects in-context knowledge induction with core agentic capabilities, including state grounding, temporal memory, planning, and adaptation in dynamic environments. Extensive evaluations across 19 state-of-the-art multimodal agents reveal significant limitations: the best-performing model, Seed-2.1-Pro, achieves a score of only 54.4/100, while other leading models (e.g., Gemini-3.1-Pro, GPT-5.6) fail to surpass 50, far below the human baseline of 83.6. Controlled comparisons further demonstrate that current agents struggle to reliably translate video exemplars into effective policies, failing to yield consistent performance gains. Ultimately, V-ICAL exposes a critical gap in the ICL capabilities of multimodal agents, underscoring an urgent need for future research.

</details>


### [44] [Global-Local Contextual Progressive Expansion Network for Martian Landslide Segmentation in Multimodal Remote Sensing Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13332)
*Leo Thomas Ramos,Sidike Paheding,Abel A. Reyes-Angulo,Rajaneesh A.,Sajinkumar K. S.,Angel D. Sappa,Thomas Oommen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对火星滑坡分割这一开放难题，系统评估了现代神经分割模型，并提出TransCPLES（U形网络，融合上下文渐进层扩展特征提取与Transformer上下文推理），在七波段多模态火星滑坡数据集MMLSv2上取得最优综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火星滑坡分割对理解其表面过程及未来空间探索具有重要意义，但目前研究不足。主要挑战在于：滑坡形态高度多变，前景区域稀疏或不规则，且轨道观测数据融合了异质的光谱与地形线索。

Method: 提出TransCPLES，一种U形网络，将上下文渐进层扩展（Contextual Progressive Layer Expansion）特征提取与基于Transformer的上下文推理相耦合，从而同时捕捉局部地貌模式与更广的空间依赖关系。基于七波段多模态火星滑坡数据集MMLSv2进行实验，并与多种卷积、注意力及Transformer分割模型对比。

Result: 在地理上分布不同的样本上评估时，TransCPLES取得最佳综合性能：对不同滑坡范围保持一致的勾画效果，前景判别稳定，并在精度与计算成本之间取得良好平衡。该研究是首个在该领域开展如此全面探索的工作。

Conclusion: 该工作为行星遥感中的深度学习研究提供参考，鼓励进一步的研究与开发。代码将在论文发表后公开。

Abstract: Automated landslide segmentation on Mars is one of the important tasks for understanding its surface processes, and all will aid in future space exploration. However, it remains a relatively underexplored open challenge because landslide morphology is highly variable, foreground regions are often sparse or irregular, and orbital observations combine heterogeneous spectral and topographic cues. In this context, this work investigates the capability of deep learning to address Martian landslide segmentation through an extensive assessment of modern neural segmentation models. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study to provide such a comprehensive exploration in this domain. We further propose TransCPLES, a U-shaped network that couples Contextual Progressive Layer Expansion feature extraction with Transformer-based contextual reasoning, enabling the model to capture local geomorphic patterns and broader spatial dependencies for more reliable landslide delineation. Experiments on MMLSv2, a seven-band multimodal Martian landslide dataset, show that TransCPLES achieves the best overall performance when evaluated on geographically distinct samples, with consistent delineation across different landslide extents, stable foreground discrimination, and a favorable balance between accuracy and computational cost compared with several state-of-the-art convolutional, attention-based, and Transformer-based segmentation models. With this work, we hope to provide a useful reference and encourage further research and development in deep learning for planetary remote sensing. Code will be available after publication.

</details>


### [45] [Query-Conditioned Spherical Centroid Aggregation for Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15335)
*Ambuj Mehrish,Anindya Nag,Sebastiano Vascon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SCALAR——一种查询条件下的自适应多模态聚合器，通过学习模态相关性权重并计算球面质心，解决对称聚合导致的性能退化问题，在五个基准中四个取得正向聚合增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态检索中的几何聚合器（Gramian 体积、双曲体积、谱目标等）对所有模态对称处理，忽略了模态间影响力的差异。在统一评测协议下，其联合得分常落后于最强单模态路径 1.9–27.6 R@1，控制变量分析将原因归结为"均匀模态影响力"。

Method: 提出 SCALAR：在计算球面质心之前，先根据查询为每个可用模态分配基于相关性的权重，从而支持任意模态子集。训练时采用掩码、降元（reduced-arity）视图，并使用秩为 8 的 LoRA 适配器，可训练参数仅 480 万。

Result: 在五个基准上，SCALAR 在其中四个取得正向聚合增益，最高达 +4.0 R@1，而所有对比的既有聚合器最多只在一个基准上为正。均匀权重消融实验复现了对称聚合的退化现象。SCALAR 在三个基准上取得最高 text-to-video R@1，第四个基准上处于最优结果的随机种子波动范围内。在测试时模态缺失场景下，其表示阶段得分在每个评测掩码率与基准上均超过已发布的 GRAM 检查点 3.2–10.9 R@1。

Conclusion: 随着模态被移除，仅在完整模态集上训练的 reranker 会逐渐趋近其纯视频路径，削弱表示层面的增益，揭示了标准两阶段检索流水线的局限性。SCALAR 表明查询条件的自适应模态加权是提升多模态聚合的有效方向。

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval integrates video, audio, subtitles, and text; however, recent geometric aggregators, such as Gramian volumes, hyperbolic volumes, and spectral objectives, treat all modalities symmetrically. Under a unified evaluation protocol, their joint scores frequently lag behind the strongest single-modality pathway by 1.9 to 27.6 R@1. Controlled analyses attribute this outcome to uniform modality influence. This work introduces Spherical Centroid Aggregation with Learned Adaptive Relevance (SCALAR), a query-conditioned aggregator that assigns relevance-based weights to each available modality before computing a spherical centroid. SCALAR accommodates arbitrary modality subsets and is trained on masked, reduced-arity views using rank-8 LoRA adapters. Across five benchmarks, SCALAR achieves positive aggregation gain on four, reaching +4.0 R@1, while none of the evaluated prior aggregators is positive on more than one. A uniform-weight ablation reproduces the degradation observed with symmetric aggregation. With only 4.8 million trainable parameters, SCALAR attains the highest text-to-video R@1 on three and performs within seed variation of the best result on a fourth. Under test-time modality dropout, SCALAR's representation-stage score surpasses the released GRAM checkpoint at every evaluated masking rate and benchmark by 3.2 to 10.9 R@1. Finally, as modalities are removed, rerankers trained exclusively on complete modality sets increasingly converge toward their video-only pathways, diminishing these representation-level gains and underscoring a limitation of standard two-stage retrieval pipelines.

</details>


### [46] [Variational Template Matching with Statistical Fusion for Anomaly Detection in Patterned Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13298)
*Qinwu Xu,Yifan Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出变分模板匹配框架，将异常模板表示为变换实例族，结合KDE密度统计得分与统一融合公式，在无训练条件下检测结构化图像异常，性能优于经典基线并与ResNet-50相当，且可显式定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化图像的小样本异常检测中，深度学习方法成本高或不实用；经典模板匹配虽简单可解释，但对尺度、旋转、透视等几何变换缺乏鲁棒性。因此需要一种兼具效率、可解释性与几何/分布鲁棒性的无训练方法。

Method: 1）变分模板匹配：将异常模板表示为变换实例族，在变换空间上通过归一化互相关进行检测；2）基于密度的统计异常得分：利用核密度估计（KDE）从局部强度分布导出，相比直方图方法更平滑、更稳健地刻画分布集中度与尾部行为；3）统一融合公式整合结构（几何相似性）与统计（分布偏差）信号，形成互补建模。

Result: 在生物细胞图像实验中，该方法优于经典基线，并在完全无训练设定下达到与ResNet-50相当的性能，同时提供显式的异常定位。

Conclusion: 该变分模板匹配与KDE统计融合的方法为结构化图像域的异常检测提供了高效、可解释且实用的解决方案，适用于深度学习方法受限的小样本场景。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in structured images is challenging in small-data settings where deep learning approaches are costly or impractical. Classical template matching is simple and interpretable but lacks robustness to geometric variations such as scale, rotation, and perspective.
  We propose a variational template matching framework that represents anomaly templates as a family of transformed instances and performs detection via normalized cross-correlation over this transformation space. To further improve robustness, we introduce a density-based statistical anomaly score derived from local intensity distributions using kernel density estimation (KDE). This produces a smooth representation that captures distributional concentration and tail behavior more robustly than histogram-based methods.
  The structural and statistical signals are integrated through a unified fusion formulation, enabling complementary modeling of geometric similarity and distributional deviation. Experiments on biological cell images demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms classical baselines and achieves competitive performance with ResNet-50 under a fully training-free setting, while providing explicit localization. The approach offers an efficient, interpretable, and practical solution for anomaly detection in structured image domains.

</details>


### [47] [Preserving Subject-Clarity in Image Outpainting with Multiscale Wavelet Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13251)
*Abhilash Neog,Taewan Kim,Yi Wu,Xu Chen,Jian Jiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向主题清晰度的图像外扩框架，将VLM引导的语义条件与多尺度小波监督相结合，并配套主题中心的数据构建流程，在四类基准上提升了主题保真度与生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业与广告图像常因构图不佳、主体被部分裁剪、文字或标识被截断及上下文不足而削弱"主题清晰度"（主体可被清晰理解的程度）。图像外扩可扩展边界并补全缺失内容，但现有基于扩散的外扩方法虽生成视觉上合理的补全结果，却因结构不一致、语义漂移或细节丢失而损害主体保真度。

Method: 提出主题清晰度外扩框架，结合VLM引导的语义条件与多尺度小波监督，以实现主体局部化的细节保持；同时构建以主体为中心的数据整理流程，从广告与自然图像中生成主体相交的外扩训练对。所设计目标不增加额外推理开销，并兼容扩散主干。

Result: 在四个广告与自然图像基准上，主题清晰度提升：以主体为中心的DreamSim误差与FID平均较匹配的监督微调分别降低3.0%和2.4%，较各数据集最强SOTA方法分别降低10.8%和7.7%。

Conclusion: 通过VLM语义引导与多尺度小波监督的结合，可在不增加推理成本的前提下有效改善扩散式外扩中的主体保真度与主题清晰度。

Abstract: Commercial and advertising images are frequently affected by poor framing, partially cropped subjects, truncated text or logos, and insufficient context, all of which can reduce subject clarity, i.e., the ability of an image to clearly communicate its primary subject. Image outpainting offers a scalable solution by extending image boundaries and recovering missing content and context. However, existing diffusion-based outpainting methods often produce visually plausible completions while degrading subject fidelity through structural inconsistencies, semantic drift, or loss of fine-grained detail. To address this limitation, we propose a subject clarity outpainting framework that combines vision-language model (VLM)-guided semantic conditioning with multiscale wavelet supervision for subject-localized detail preservation. To support training, we develop a subject-centric data curation pipeline that constructs subject-intersecting outpainting pairs from advertising and natural images. The resulting objective introduces no additional inference cost and is designed to be compatible with diffusion-based backbones. Across four advertising and natural-image benchmarks, our method improves subject clarity, reducing subject-centered DreamSim error and FID on average by 3.0% and 2.4% over matched supervised fine-tuning, and by 10.8% and 7.7% over the strongest state-of-the-art approach per dataset, respectively.

</details>


### [48] [SignMimic: Robust High-Quality Sign Language Motion Generation via Human-Shape-Oblivious Pose Transfer Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14122)
*Zhewen He,Junyi Yu,Haomian Huang,Zhenhua Li,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SignMimic 通过刚体规范化、非刚体适配与补全的分解式流水线，实现手语视频模仿，在多个大规模数据集上达到领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语视频模仿任务需在保留身份与语言形式的同时复现源动作。已有流水线将刚体运动、非刚体形变与视角相关补全纠缠在单一生成器中，导致手形漂移和时空不稳定。

Method: 提出 SignMimic，分三步：(i) 基于 TNet 的 SE(3) 刚体规范化以稳定全局姿态；(ii) 在规范化空间进行非刚体适配，借助 NIF2D 保留手/脸等细粒度关节与协同发音；(iii) 采用 Pose-MAE 风格补全，再进行条件视频扩散。该分解注入了几何与语言先验。

Result: 在 ASL 50K、How2Sign、CSL News 等大规模数据集上，SignMimic 在视频质量、身份相似度和帧连续性上达到先进水平，同时在生成视频上做反向翻译（SLT）时损失最小。消融实验验证了刚体规范化、非刚体适配与补全各自的作用。

Conclusion: 将刚体运动、非刚体形变与补全显式分解并注入几何与语言先验，可有效提升手语视频模仿的形状与时空一致性；代码、模型权重与示例视频将开源。

Abstract: We study the challenge of sign language video mimicking: given a driving video and a single reference frame, synthesize a video where the target signer reproduces the source
  motion while preserving identity and linguistic form. Prior pipelines entangle rigid motion, non-rigid deformation, and view-dependent completion in a monolithic generator,
  causing handshape drift and spatio-temporal instability. We present SignMimic, which (i) applies a TNet-based model to study SE(3) rigid canonicalization to stabilize global
  pose, (ii) performs non-rigid adaptation in a canonical space to preserve fine-grained articulators (hands/face) and coarticulation via NIF2D, and (iii) uses Pose-MAE-style
  completion before conditional video diffusion. This factorization injects geometric and linguistic priors, yielding shape and spatio-temporal consistency. On several large-scale
  datasets (ASL 50K, How2Sign, CSL News), SignMimic achieves state-of-the-art-level performance on video quality, identity similarity, and frame continuity while also achieving
  minimal loss when performing back translation (SLT) on generated videos. Ablations confirm the role of rigid canonicalization, non-rigid adaptation, and completion. Code, model
  checkpoints, and video examples will be released.

</details>


### [49] [PC$^2$-AD: Point Cloud Upsampling to Safeguard 3D Anomaly Detection with Resolution-constrained Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14722)
*Yutong Gu,Yingxi Xie,Kejin Huang,Jian Ning,Hanzhe Liang,Linlin Shen,Jinbao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PC²-AD点云上采样框架，在检测前对稀疏测试点云进行补偿，缓解训练-测试分辨率差异，提升多种3D异常检测器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘部署中低成本、低分辨率传感器产生的测试点云比训练数据稀疏得多，这种训练-测试采样分辨率差距改变了3D异常检测器可用的局部几何信息，导致检测性能下降。

Method: PC²-AD包含三个模块：1）目标域候选生成（TCG），将预训练上采样器适配到正常训练几何并生成稠密候选池；2）几何感知候选过滤（GACF），根据几何间距和空间覆盖率选择候选点；3）保常态点补偿（NPPC），通过比较候选点与其输入锚点的常态性得分进行精炼。选中点与未改变的输入点合并后送入现有检测器。

Result: 在Anomaly-ShapeNet的两个设置和Real3D-AD上，使用六种检测器进行实验。在Anomaly-ShapeNet每个设置下六种检测器的对象级与点级AUROC均值均有提升，Real3D-AD上有四种检测器提升。

Conclusion: 点云补偿作为一种输入级方法，可有效改善低分辨率传感条件下的3D异常检测性能。代码已公开。

Abstract: Low-cost and low-resolution sensors used in edge deployments can produce test point clouds that are substantially sparser than the normal training data. This train-test sampling-resolution gap changes the local geometry available to a 3D anomaly detector. We propose PC$^2$-AD, a point cloud upsampling framework that compensates sparse test inputs before downstream detection. Target Domain Candidate Generation (TCG) adapts a pretrained upsampler to normal training geometry and generates a dense candidate pool. Geometry-Aware Candidate Filtering (GACF) selects candidates according to geometric spacing and spatial coverage. Normality-Preserving Point Compensation (NPPC) refines the selection by comparing candidate normality scores with those of their input anchors. The selected points are combined with the unchanged input points and processed by the existing detector. Experiments with six detectors on two Anomaly-ShapeNet settings and Real3D-AD show improvements in the mean of object-level and point-level AUROC for all six detectors in each Anomaly-ShapeNet setting and four on Real3D-AD. These results support point cloud compensation as an input-level approach to improving 3D anomaly detection under low-resolution sensing conditions. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/gyutong406-commits/PC2-AD.

</details>


### [50] [(How) Do MLLMs Report Bistable Images Like Humans?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13254)
*Ryota Takatsuki,Tomoki Doi,Amane Watahiki,Anil K. Seth,Hitomi Yanaka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究以鸭子-兔子等双稳态图像为刺激，检验多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）是否表现出类似人类的报告行为及其内部计算机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双稳态图像（如鸭子-兔子）在人类中通常一次仅报告一种互不相容的解释。作者希望探究MLLMs是否呈现类似的报告行为，并揭示其背后的内部计算机制，同时缓解记忆混淆问题。

Method: 采用LLaVA系列模型，研究两个可处理的维度：可调制性（报告能否被自下而上的视觉线索和自上而下的语言先验所偏置）与排他性（回答是否仅承诺单一解释）。在经典鸭子-兔子图与合成的Visual Anagrams上均进行测试，以缓解记忆混淆。

Result: 行为层面，视觉与语言操纵均以与人类一致的方式系统性地改变模型报告，且回答仍以排他性为主。机制层面，这些效应源于相互竞争的图像token表征、自下而上与自上而下调制的不同通路，以及排他性报告与物体计数编码之间的联系。

Conclusion: MLLMs在双稳态图像上表现出与人类相似的可调制性与排他性报告行为，其背后由竞争性图像表征、分离的调制通路及物体计数编码共同支撑。

Abstract: Bistable images such as the duck-rabbit are classic stimuli in which one image supports multiple mutually incompatible interpretations, typically reported one at a time in humans. We ask whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs) show similar report behavior and what internal computations support it. Using the LLaVA family, we study two tractable dimensions: modulability, whether reports can be biased by bottom-up visual cues and top-down linguistic priors, and exclusivity, whether responses commit to a single interpretation. We test both on the canonical duck-rabbit and on synthetic Visual Anagrams to mitigate memorization confounds. Behaviorally, both visual and linguistic manipulations systematically shift reports in human-consistent ways, while responses remain predominantly exclusive. Mechanistically, these effects arise from competing image-token representations, distinct pathways for bottom-up and top-down modulation, and a link between exclusive reporting and object-count encoding. Code and data are available at https://github.com/rtakatsky/mllm-bistable-images.

</details>


### [51] [Bridging the Synthetic-to-Real Gap for Few-Shot Cryo-ET Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14097)
*Siddhant Bharadwaj,Ashish Vashist,Rashi Singh,Pranav Vinodh,Nishanth Artham,Runmin Jiang,Xingjian Li,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可学习变换模块的合成到真实域适应框架，在输入与特征双层面弥合域间差异，提升少样本冷冻电镜子断层分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷冻电子断层扫描（cryo-ET）中的子断层分类因标注样本稀缺而困难；虽可用模拟器生成无限合成数据，但合成与真实子断层间存在显著域间差异，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 提出一种新颖的合成到真实域适应框架，引入可学习变换模块，在输入层面与特征层面同时弥合合成域与真实域之间的鸿沟。

Result: 大量实验表明，在少样本设置下，所提方法持续优于现有迁移学习基线。

Conclusion: 该框架有效缓解合成-真实域间差异，验证了在少样本cryo-ET子断层分类中的有效性与优越性。

Abstract: Subtomogram classification in cryo-electron tomography (cryo-ET) is a challenging problem due to the scarcity of labeled examples. While cryo-ET simulators can be adopted to generate unlimited synthetic data, the substantial domain gap between synthetic and real subtomograms hinders its practical utilization. In this work, we propose a novel synthetic-to-real adaptation framework with a learnable transformation module, bridging this gap at both the input and feature levels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms existing transfer learning baselines in few-shot settings.

</details>


### [52] [MoVT: Video-Augmented Motion Tokenizer for Text-to-Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14965)
*Beibei Jing,Tianle Guo,Youjia Zhang,Zikai Song,Yawei Luo,Junqing Yu,Tao Guan,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoVT 提出跨模态增强运动 tokenizer，将离散 3D 运动 token 投影到 2D 域，借助视频数据丰富运动码本，再映射回 3D 域以对齐 2D/3D 码本，并结合生成式掩码 Transformer 提升文本到运动生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本驱动的 3D 人体运动生成受限于 3D 运动训练数据不足，难以应对多样且不受约束的文本提示。作者希望利用大量人体动作视频来弥补 3D 数据稀缺问题。

Method: 核心是跨模态增强运动 tokenizer：先将离散 3D 运动 token 投影到 2D 域，用视频中的复杂真实运动模式丰富运动码本；再将增强后的离散 token 映射回 3D 域，得到对齐且表达能力更强的 3D 与 2D 码本。增强码本被集成到生成式掩码 Transformer 中，以模态无关方式预测被掩码的运动 token 索引，从而可利用由 2D 码本和带标注运动视频生成的文本-索引对来进一步增强生成器。

Result: 大量实证评估表明，MoVT 在多个关键指标上优于先前的最先进方法。

Conclusion: MoVT 通过跨模态 2D-3D 码本增强与模态无关的掩码生成建模，有效利用了视频数据，缓解了 3D 运动数据不足的问题，并在文本到运动生成任务上取得领先表现。

Abstract: Text-driven 3D human motion generation models face significant challenges in responding to diverse and unconstrained textual prompts, primarily due to the limited availability of 3D motion training data. To address this, we introduce MoVT, a novel framework that effectively leverages the extensive range of human action videos to enhance text-to-motion generation. At the core of our approach is the cross-modal augmented motion tokenizer, which projects discrete 3D motion tokens into the 2D domain. This projection allows us to enrich the motion codebook with complex, real-world motion patterns derived from videos. The enriched discrete tokens are then mapped back to the 3D domain, resulting in aligned 3D and 2D codebooks with an enhanced capacity to represent intricate motions. These enhanced codebooks are integrated into a generative masked transformer, which predicts masked motion token indices in a modality-agnostic manner. This enables the use of text-index pairs, generated from the 2D codebook and annotated motion videos, to further enhance the generator. Extensive empirical evaluations show that MoVT performs favorably against prior state-of-the-art methods across multiple key metrics.

</details>


### [53] [Physically Typed and Geometry-Aware Representations for Earth Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13868)
*Rajiv Ranjan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该提案提出一个分阶段的证伪研究计划，检验通用地球基础模型是否需要显式保留物理场的几何类型（标量、极向量、轴向量、余向量、高阶张量）及其在旋转/反射/坐标变换下的变换律，而非依赖传统嵌入加数据增强。先以ERA5干跑对比四类变体，仅在显式几何分型带来可复现增益时，才推进到多模态地球基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测基础模型擅长学习高维语义地理空间嵌入，天气气候模型则证明地球专用几何、球面算子、网格与混合物理求解器可显著提升预测，但二者并不等价。常规潜在嵌入没有内在物理变换律，而标量场、切向极向量场、轴/赝向量、余向量和高阶张量在旋转、反射及局部坐标架变化下变换方式各异。核心争议是：通用地球基础模型应显式保留这些区分，还是标准嵌入加数据增强已能学到所有关键信息。

Method: 提出分阶段证伪程序，而非预设更复杂架构。首先进行算力友好的ERA5干跑，在空间、时间、取向与低数据偏移条件下，比较四类变体：常规嵌入、数据增强匹配版本、类型化等变版本、Hodge/Helmholtz版本。仅当显式几何分型产生可复现改进时，才推进至多模态地球基础模型，使语义嵌入与物理类型化场共存。

Result: 尚无最终实验结果；计划通过ERA5干跑获得可复现对比证据，判断显式几何分型是否优于增强匹配基线。提案强调当前缺口界定更窄且更可辩护：已有系统尊重球面域几何，新兴工作也在球面上显式学习标量/向量场；尚未解决的是基础模型规模、多模态、宇称感知的场类型化能否带来超越既有方法的实际增益。

Conclusion: 该工作将问题从“当前模型是否完全忽略几何”收窄为“基础模型规模下的宇称感知场类型化是否产生实用增益”，并以分阶段证伪降低架构假设风险。若显式几何分型在受控偏移下稳定优于增强基线，则为多模态地球基础模型中的物理类型化嵌入提供依据；否则支持标准嵌入加增强已足够。

Abstract: Earth-observation (EO) foundation models have become exceptionally effective at learning se mantic, high-dimensional geospatial embeddings, while modern weather and climate models have demonstrated that Earth-specific geometry, spherical operators, meshes, and hybrid physical solvers can materially improve prediction. Yet these two advances are not equivalent. A conventional latent embedding has no inherent physical transformation law, whereas scalar fields, tangent polar-vector fields, axial/pseudovector quantities, covectors, and higher-order tensors transform differently under rotations, reflections, and changes of local coordinate frame. This proposal asks whether a general purpose Earth foundation model should preserve those distinctions explicitly, or whether standard embeddings plus augmentation already learn everything that matters. The central contribution is therefore not a more complicated architecture by assumption, but a staged falsification program. A compute-conscious ERA5 dry run first compares conventional, augmentation-matched, typed equivariant, and Hodge/Helmholtz variants under spatial, temporal, orientation, and low-data shifts. Only if explicit geometric typing yields reproducible improvements does the program advance toward a multimodal Earth foundation model in which semantic embeddings coexist with physically typed fields. The proposed gap is narrower and more defensible than claiming that current models ignore geometry entirely: several systems already respect spherical domain geometry, and emerging work explicitly learns scalar/vector fields on spheres. The unresolved question is whether foundation-scale, multimodal, parity-aware field typing produces practical gains beyond those existing approaches.

</details>


### [54] [PuzzleMate: Benchmarking MLLMs for Egocentric Puzzle Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14473)
*Avijit Dasgupta,Shayon Dasgupta,Zakaria Laskar,C. V. Jawahar,Karteek Alahari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文以拼图任务为战略性测试平台，提出PuzzleMate框架（第一人称视角）评测多模态大模型作为具身AI助手的能力。用户参与式研究揭示七个关键瓶颈，并据此构建基准。结果显示GPT-5.2、Gemini-2.5-Pro等先进模型在精细空间推理与序列逻辑上存在显著性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人AI助手有望从数字界面演化为具身伙伴，指导用户完成复杂物理活动。这要求助手不仅能识别物体，还需提供与用户实时进度一致的精确分步指令。尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在通用视觉理解上表现良好，其在细粒度操作任务中提供有根据的、序列化指导的能力尚未得到验证。

Method: 选择拼图作为战略性测试平台，因其要求高精度空间推理、区分细微几何差异以及严格遵循序列逻辑。提出PuzzleMate框架，在第一人称（egocentric）视角下采集拼图解题过程，并采用用户参与式（user-in-the-loop）研究，评估最先进MLLMs对当前拼图状态的感知能力及可执行下一步指令的生成质量。

Result: 分析揭示七项限制MLLM有效性的关键瓶颈。在此基础上提出一个基准，用于系统评估MLLMs的拼图解题推理能力。实验发现当前GPT-5.2、Gemini-2.5-Pro等模型存在显著性能差距：尽管能力较强，仍难以应对拼图辅助所需的复杂推理与序列逻辑。

Conclusion: MLLMs在细粒度、序列化的物理操作指导任务上仍存在明显不足；PuzzleMate框架与所提基准为评测和改进具身AI助手的逐步指导能力提供了方法论与评估工具。

Abstract: Personal AI assistants hold the potential to evolve from digital interfaces into embodied companions capable of guiding users through complex physical activities. For these assistants to become integral to daily life, they must do more than identify objects; they must provide precise, step-by-step instructions that align with a user's real-time progress. While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show promise in general visual understanding, their ability to deliver grounded, sequential guidance for fine-grained manipulation tasks remains largely unverified.
  In this paper, we choose the jigsaw puzzle as a strategic testbed for this capability. Unlike general object recognition, puzzle solving demands high-precision spatial reasoning, the ability to distinguish between minute geometric variations, and a rigorous adherence to sequential logic.
  We investigate this capability through PuzzleMate, a novel framework focused on jigsaw puzzle solving captured through an egocentric viewpoint. We deploy PuzzleMate in a user-in-the-loop study to evaluate how well state-of-the-art MLLMs perceive the current puzzle state and generate actionable next-step instructions. Our analysis reveals seven key bottlenecks that limit their effectiveness. Building on these insights, we propose a benchmark that enables systematic evaluation of MLLMs' reasoning capabilities for puzzle solving. Our findings reveal a substantial performance gap in current models like GPT-5.2 and Gemini-2.5-Pro; while these MLLMs are highly capable, they struggle to navigate the intricate reasoning and sequential logic essential for jigsaw puzzle assistance.

</details>


### [55] [RIGOR: Rig-Informed Geometry for Omnidirectional Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13504)
*Tingjun Huang,Dmitry Rudshin,Mathieu Meyer,Pietro Bonazzi,Marc Pollefeys,Emilia Szymańska*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RIGOR：面向重力对齐全景视频的大规模重建管线，冻结前馈透视主干，将全景视为四视图虚拟rig，实现局部预测修复、回环检测与几何验证，并用Sim(3)位姿图优化消除漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈式3D重建虽可仅凭图像流恢复稠密场景表示与相机运动，但在长轨迹及重复结构、弱纹理、动态物体等困难环境中易出现不一致；全景相机虽能提供更宽覆盖与更丰富信息，但多数模型不支持360度图像或需额外微调。因此需弥合二者的鸿沟。

Method: 保留冻结的前馈透视主干，将每张全景图作为四视图虚拟rig：利用rig结构检测并修复局部不一致预测；通过循环四视图一致性检索回环闭合；在全局优化前对候选重访进行几何验证；将验证后的约束构建Sim(3)位姿图，纠正累积的旋转、平移与尺度漂移。

Result: 在具有挑战性的建筑工地序列上，所提一致性机制相较前馈基线同时提升了轨迹精度与重建几何质量。代码已开源。

Conclusion: RIGOR以无需微调的方式将冻结的透视前馈重建主干扩展至重力对齐全景视频，通过四视图虚拟rig的一致性机制与Sim(3)位姿图优化有效抑制长轨迹漂移，提升轨迹与几何精度。

Abstract: Recent developments in feed-forward 3D reconstruction resulted in models which can recover dense scene representations and camera motion solely from an image stream. However, such predictions are prone to becoming inconsistent over long trajectories, specifically in demanding environments with repetitive structures, weak textures and dynamic objects or people. One way to mitigate those challenges is to use an omnidirectional camera, which provides wide spatial coverage and captures richer visual information. Yet, the majority of models do not offer support for 360-degree imagery or require additional fine-tuning. To bridge these two aspects, we present RIGOR: a large-scale reconstruction pipeline for gravity-aligned omnidirectional videos that retains a frozen feed-forward perspective backbone and exploits each panorama as a four-view virtual rig. The rig structure is used to detect and repair locally inconsistent predictions, to retrieve loop closures through cyclic four-view consensus, and to geometrically verify candidate revisits before global optimization. Verified constraints drive a Sim(3) pose graph that corrects accumulated rotation, translation, and scale drift along the sequence. We demonstrate that the proposed consistency mechanisms improve both trajectory accuracy and reconstructed geometry over a feed-forward baseline on challenging construction-site sequences. The code is made available under this link: https://github.com/TangentH/RIGOR.

</details>


### [56] [Small Object Detection in Drone Aerial Imagery with LAF-YOLOv10](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14560)
*Quratulain Nayeem,Fahmina Taranum,Mohammed Mudassir Uddin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究直接检验了"独立验证有效的架构技巧组合后增益可叠加"这一假设：将四项技术集成到 YOLOv10n 得到 LAF-YOLOv10，在 VisDrone-DET2019 上训练与评测。结果显示组合并不成立，性能反而比原始 YOLOv10n 低 7.8 个 mAP@0.5 点，且该缺陷可迁移至 UAVDT，并被消融定位到 P2 替代 P5 检测头与 PC-C2f 弱化骨干之间的特定交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用目标检测器在 UAV 航拍图像上精度显著下降：目标往往仅有几个像素，且机载算力有限。既有工作通常把各自独立验证过的架构技术直接拼装成一个检测器，隐含假设"单独有效的技术组合后仍能带来叠加增益"。本文旨在直接压力测试这一可组合性假设，检验组合效应是否等于各组件单独贡献之和。

Method: 将四项技术集成到 YOLOv10n：(1) 部分卷积 C2f 骨干块（PC-C2f）；(2) 注意力引导特征金字塔网络（AG-FPN）；(3) 用 P2 检测头替换大目标 P5 头；(4) Wise-IoU v3 回归损失。在 VisDrone-DET2019 上以 3 个随机种子（42、123、256）训练三次，与未修改的 YOLOv10n 对比；采用 TIDE 误差分解、逐类别分析、逐组件消融、注意力与损失对比、向 UAVDT 的零样本迁移，以及留出集/test-dev 评估来定位组合成功或失败之处。

Result: LAF-YOLOv10 在 2.14M 参数下仅达到 24.0±0.4% mAP@0.5，比 YOLOv10n（31.8%）低 7.8 个点；该缺陷迁移至 UAVDT（-10.0 点），并被留出集与 test-dev 评估确认（23.5%、22.5%）。背景误检、定位误差和重复检测均朝 AG-FPN 与 Wise-IoU 设计意图的反方向移动。消融将缺陷溯源到具体来源：P2 替代 P5 检测头独立造成 2.5 点损失，叠加于被 PC-C2f 削弱的骨干之上时又产生 2.5 点交互惩罚；PC-C2f 自身的 2.0 点损失与部分预训练权值移植（73/150 骨干张量迁移）一致。

Conclusion: 组合性在此并不成立，失败可归因于一个特定交互（P2/P5 头替换与 PC-C2f 弱化骨干的耦合），而非四个组件各自单独失效。结论是：架构技巧的可组合性必须被直接验证，而不能预设；这也提示在 UAV 小目标检测中，需系统评估组件间交互而非依赖孤立增益的简单叠加。

Abstract: General-purpose object detectors lose accuracy on UAV footage, where targets span only a handful of pixels and onboard compute is limited. Prior work composes independently-validated architectural techniques into one detector, assuming gains reported in isolation transfer once combined. We stress-test that assumption directly. LAF-YOLOv10 integrates four techniques into YOLOv10n: a Partial Convolution C2f (PC-C2f) backbone block, an Attention-Guided Feature Pyramid Network (AG-FPN), a P2 detection head replacing the large-object P5 head, and Wise-IoU v3 regression, asking whether their combined effect matches what each contributes alone. We train LAF-YOLOv10 three times (seeds 42, 123, 256) on VisDrone-DET2019, benchmark against unmodified YOLOv10n, and use TIDE error decomposition, per-category breakdown, per-component ablation, attention/loss comparisons, zero-shot transfer to UAVDT, and held-out/test-dev evaluation to localize where the combination succeeds or fails. Composability does not hold here. LAF-YOLOv10 reaches 24.0+/-0.4% mAP@0.5 at 2.14M parameters, 7.8 points below YOLOv10n (31.8%), a deficit that transfers to UAVDT (-10.0 points) and is confirmed by held-out and test-dev evaluation (23.5%, 22.5%). Background false positives, localization error, and duplicate detections move in the direction AG-FPN and Wise-IoU were designed to push. Ablation traces the deficit to a specific source: the P2/-P5 head swap costs 2.5 points independently plus a 2.5-point interaction penalty when layered onto a backbone weakened by PC-C2f, whose own 2.0-point loss is consistent with a partial pretrained-weight transplant (73/150 backbone tensors transfer). The failure is attributable to a specific interaction, not the four components individually. Composability must be verified directly, not assumed. Code/checkpoints: https://github.com/Mudassiruddin7/Small-Object-Detection-in-UAV-Imagery.

</details>


### [57] [EECTracker: Swarm Motion Prior-Guided Feature Compensation for Airborne Optical UAV Swarm Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15171)
*Zhaochen Chu,Tao Song,Ren Jin,Mingdong Jia,Defu Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EECTracker，一种群运动先验引导的联合检测-跟踪框架，用于机载光学无人机集群跟踪，通过能量-熵一致性激活实现特征补偿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机载光学跟踪无人机集群面临目标尺度极小、视角快速变化和背景杂乱等挑战，导致目标特征响应减弱、检测器响应间歇性缺失；现有MOT方法依赖可靠的目标检测响应维持状态与身份，响应不可靠时轨迹碎片化和身份切换严重。

Method: 从可靠的历史轨迹片段构建概率性群运动先验，捕捉集群共享的短期像平面运动趋势及其不确定性，为跨帧特征补偿提供空间引导；在此基础上提出能-熵一致性激活（EEC Activation），利用特征残差能量与局部残差熵评估运动先验条件下的特征一致性，并以其Local EEC分数指导像素级特征补偿：增强潜在目标区域中与运动先验一致的特征响应，抑制不一致的背景响应。

Result: 在AIRMOT和UAVSwarm数据集上取得优于当前先进方法的整体跟踪性能；相比最强竞争方法SCT-MOT，AIRMOT上MOTA/IDF1分别提升3.89/1.79个百分点，UAVSwarm上提升2.81/1.74个百分点，同时保持在线推理速度。

Conclusion: 通过引入群运动先验引导的特征补偿机制，EECTracker有效缓解检测响应不可靠导致的轨迹碎片化与身份切换，在无人机集群跟踪任务上实现性能与效率的平衡。

Abstract: Airborne optical tracking of uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV) swarms is challenging due to extremely small target scales, rapid viewpoint changes, and cluttered backgrounds, which can weaken target feature responses and lead to intermittent or temporarily missing detector responses. Existing multi-object tracking methods generally depend on reliable target-specific detector responses to maintain target states and identities across frames. When such responses become unreliable, target states cannot be reliably updated and cross-frame association cues become ambiguous, resulting in fragmented trajectories and identity switches. To address this problem, we propose EECTracker, a swarm-motion-prior-guided joint detection-and-tracking framework for airborne optical UAV swarm tracking. EECTracker constructs a probabilistic swarm motion prior from reliable historical tracklets to capture the shared short-term image-plane motion tendency of the swarm and its uncertainty, providing spatial guidance for cross-frame feature compensation. Building on this prior, we introduce Energy--Entropy Consistency Activation (EEC Activation) to evaluate motion-prior-conditioned feature consistency using feature residual energy and local residual entropy. The resulting Local EEC score guides pixel-level feature compensation by enhancing motion-prior-consistent feature responses in potential target regions while suppressing inconsistent background responses. Experiments on AIRMOT and UAVSwarm show that EECTracker achieves superior overall tracking performance compared with state-of-the-art methods. Compared with the strongest competing method SCT-MOT, EECTracker improves MOTA/IDF1 by 3.89/1.79 percentage points on AIRMOT and by 2.81/1.74 percentage points on UAVSwarm, while maintaining online inference speed.

</details>


### [58] [Forward-Facing Near-Infrared Adds Little to Colour for Farm-Machinery Traversability: A Site-Disjoint Evaluation of Sensor-Dependent Spatial Leakage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13265)
*Sungwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 消除空间数据泄漏后，近红外（NIR）成像在日间农业可通行性任务上并不优于普通彩色相机；此前的NIR优势源于序列级划分导致的空间自相关泄漏，属于站点特异性伪象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有基准认为NIR在农业可通行性上占优，但其采用序列级数据划分，使空间自相关的影像进入测试集，人为抬高NIR性能（尤其在困难的田埂边界分割上）。作者希望厘清该优势是否真实、是否可泛化，以避免误导传感器选型与采购决策。

Method: 在AI Hub自动驾驶语料上，采用严格按录制站点留出的评估协议；对比四种配置：彩色、NIR、亮度对照及其融合；覆盖五类可通行性类别，并进行留出站点轮换实验。

Result: 在严格留出站点评估下，四种配置在五类可通行性类别上均未能稳定超越标准彩色。单一留出站点上出现的性能提升在轮换留出站点后完全无法复现，说明其为站点特异性伪象。校正空间泄漏后，NIR的表观领先被完全消除，而非对所有传感器类型造成均匀的性能下降。

Conclusion: 为日间农业车辆加装前向NIR相机仍缺乏证据支持；受位置泄漏影响的评估会扭曲传感器排序并误导采购决策。

Abstract: Near-infrared (NIR) imaging does not consistently outperform standard color cameras for daytime agricultural traversability once spatial data leakage is eliminated. Prior benchmarks suggesting an NIR advantage used sequence-level splits that permitted spatially autocorrelated imagery into test sets, artificially inflating NIR performance, especially on difficult paddy-boundary segmentation.
  When evaluated across strictly held-out recording sites using the AI Hub autonomous driving corpus, none of the four tested configurations (color, NIR, a luminance control, or their fusion) reliably surpasses standard color across any of the five traversability classes. Furthermore, apparent performance gains observed at a single held-out site completely fail to replicate when the held-out site is rotated, demonstrating that the observed benefits were site-specific artifacts rather than generalizable improvements.
  Correcting for spatial leakage erases the apparent lead of NIR entirely rather than uniformly degrading performance across all sensor types. Consequently, adding a forward-facing NIR camera to a daylight agricultural vehicle sensor suite remains unproven, and evaluations compromised by location leakage risk distorting sensor rankings and misleading procurement decisions.

</details>


### [59] [ViCo-SAM3: Vision-Conditioned Alignment for Open-Vocabulary Camouflaged Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15418)
*Qiangqiang Zhou,Wenjun Tang,Yong Chen,Dandan Zhu,Jiawei Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ViCo-SAM3框架，通过视觉条件化对齐缓解开放词汇伪装目标分割中文本语义与像素级视觉线索的语义鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇伪装目标分割（OVCOS）需在文本引导下分割未见过的伪装目标。作者观察到SAM3在此任务中仍存在全局文本语义与细粒度像素级视觉线索之间的显著语义鸿沟；同时，全量微调文本编码器带来庞大参数开销，并易过拟合训练类别，削弱开放词汇表征的灵活性。

Method: 提出ViCo-SAM3视觉条件化对齐框架。核心为ViCo模块，用全局视觉上下文动态调制文本嵌入，使文本表征适配当前图像内容以桥接视觉-文本语义鸿沟；并设计ViCoBind（视觉条件化跨模态绑定）模块，增强视觉与文本表征间的跨模态交互与语义对齐。

Result: 在OVCamo基准上取得state-of-the-art性能，并展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过视觉条件化调制文本嵌入并强化跨模态绑定，可在不显著增加参数、避免过拟合的前提下有效缓解语义鸿沟，为OVCOS提供简洁且泛化性强的解决方案。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary camouflaged object segmentation (OVCOS) aims to segment unseen camouflaged objects under text guidance. We observe that SAM3 still suffers from a pronounced semantic gap between global textual semantics and fine-grained pixel-level visual cues in OVCOS. Meanwhile, fully fine-tuning the text encoder introduces heavy parameter overhead and risks overfitting to training categories, which compromises open-vocabulary representation flexibility. To address these issues, we propose ViCo-SAM3, a Vision-Conditioned alignment framework designed for OVCOS. Specifically, we introduce vision-conditioned (ViCo) module, which dynamically modulates text embeddings with global visual context, enabling textual representations to adapt to the current image content and thereby effectively bridging the semantic gap between vision and text. Building on this, we further design a vision-conditioned cross-modal binding (ViCoBind) module to enhance cross-modal interaction and semantic alignment between visual and textual representations. Without bells and whistles, ViCo-SAM3 achieves state-of-the-art performance on the OVCamo benchmark and demonstrates strong generalization.

</details>


### [60] [Talking to Me or Someone Else? Rethinking Talk-to-Me Detection in Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14118)
*Feiyu Du,Xi He,Jia Li,Yapeng Tian,Weili Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将"与我说话(TTM)"识别从离线片段级二分类重构为在线帧级多状态预测任务，构建了含406个片段、约90万帧标注的Online TTM Dataset，并提出融合音、视、语音语义多模态社会线索的模型，在TTM上取得75.5%帧级F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTM研究多被定义为离线片段级识别，与在线交互场景不匹配，且忽略了第一视角视频中自然出现的多种非TTM说话状态（如与他人说话、自言自语、背景等），导致问题建模过于简化。

Method: 将TTM重新表述为在线帧级预测任务，不将其视为对单一负类的二分类，而是在多样非TTM状态下进行建模；基于Ego4D社会交互基准扩展构建Online TTM Dataset（406个第一视角片段、约90万帧，逐帧标注背景、TTM、与他人说话、自言自语等类别）；在该基准上评估五个适配基线，并提出融合音频、视觉与语音语义线索的多模态新模型。

Result: 所提多模态模型联合利用音频、视觉和语音语义线索，在TTM上达到75.5%的帧级F1，优于强基线，并支持系统分析不同说话状态对TTM识别的影响。

Conclusion: 将TTM识别重构为在线帧级、多非TTM状态的预测问题更具现实意义；新数据集与多模态方法验证了该表述的有效性，并为第一视角社会交互理解提供了可分析的基准。

Abstract: Online understanding of who is talking to the camera wearer is a key capability for egocentric social interaction. However, existing talk-to-me (TTM) studies are commonly formulated as offline clip-level recognition, which is poorly aligned with online interaction and overlooks the diverse non-TTM speaking states that naturally arise in egocentric videos. In this paper, we revisit this problem by reformulating it as an online, frame-level prediction task. Instead of treating TTM as a binary problem against a single negative class, we model it in the presence of diverse and previously underexplored non-TTM states, such as talking-to-others, self-talking, and background conditions. To support this new formulation, we construct an Online TTM Dataset consisting of 406 egocentric video clips with approximately 900K annotated frames, each labeled with frame-level social interaction categories (e.g., background, TTM, talking-to-others, self-talking), by extending the Ego4D social interaction benchmark. In this benchmark, we evaluate five adapted baselines and develop a new model that integrates social cues across modalities. Experimental results show that our multimodal model, which jointly leverages audio, visual, and speech-semantic cues, achieves 75.5% frame-level F1 on TTM, outperforming strong baselines and enabling a systematic analysis of how different speaking states affect TTM recognition.

</details>


### [61] [Filling the Unseen: Scene Extrapolation via 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13262)
*Yunlai Zhou,Yiren Lu,Tuo Liang,Disheng Liu,Vipin Chaudhary,Yu Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向3D Gaussian Splatting外推与插值的整体框架：通过独立相机视角检测实现并行无冲突外推，避免"生成-重建-位移"循环的误差累积，并用层次化流程分别处理独立与依赖视角；同时提出即插即用的质量感知掩码（QA-Mask），依据像素级渲染质量校准学习权重，避免生成数据破坏已重建良好区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D Gaussian Splatting在训练视角分布内可实现逼真重建，但在分布外新视角上性能下降，出现未观测区域空洞与可观测区域伪影。现有工作将其建模为外推与插值并用生成模型处理，但外推尺度与质量受限；且其反复执行"生成-重建-位移"循环，每步依赖前一步结果，导致误差逐步累积。此外，已有方法忽视生成图像与原始图像之间的不一致，损害了原本重建良好的区域。

Method: 1）设计独立相机视角检测机制，实现并行、无冲突的外推，摆脱对易出错循环的依赖；2）在此基础上构建层次化流程，分别对独立视角与依赖视角进行外推；3）提出即插即用的质量感知掩码（QA-Mask）模块，通过像素级渲染质量校准学习权重，选择性使用生成数据，防止生成引入的退化破坏已良好重建区域。

Result: 大量实验证明该框架性能优越；QA-Mask可泛化到多种生成式重建模型。

Conclusion: 该工作以并行无冲突外推与层次化处理替代误差累积的循环范式，并通过QA-Mask实现生成数据的质量选择性利用，为3DGS的分布外新视角外推与插值提供了整体性解决方案，且模块具备良好通用性。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting achieves photorealistic reconstruction within training view distribution, yet it degrades on out-of-distribution novel views, exhibiting holes in unobserved regions and artifacts in observable areas. Recent works formulate this task as extrapolation and interpolation and try to address it with generative models, but remain limited in extrapolation scale and quality. They repeat a generate-reconstruct-shift cycle to progressively build a scene, which introduces accumulated errors with every step conditioning on previous outcomes. In this work, we propose a holistic framework for extrapolation and interpolation. We devise an independent camera view detection mechanism to enable parallel conflict-free extrapolation, circumventing the reliance on the aforementioned error-prone cycle. Building upon this, we design a hierarchical pipeline that extrapolates independent and dependent camera views separately. Additionally, previous methods overlook inconsistency between generated and original images, resulting in compromising well-reconstructed areas. We propose a plug-and-play Quality-Aware Mask (QA-Mask) module, enabling selective utilization of generated data. By calibrating learning weights with pixel-wise rendering quality, it prevents generation-induced degradations on well-constructed areas. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior performance of our framework, with QA-Mask generalizing to multiple generative reconstruction models.

</details>


### [62] [From Visual Feedback to Textual Reviews: A Multi-Agent Vision-Language Framework for Image-Grounded Review Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14761)
*Utsav Kumar Nareti,Ayush Bansal,Kumari Priya,Chandranath Adak,Soumi Chattopadhyay,Muhammad Saqib,Saeed Anwar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"基于图像的评价辅助"新任务：从用户上传的商品图片生成可编辑的评论草稿，并构建含四个专职角色的多智能体视觉-语言框架，在Amazon Reviews Electronics子集上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商平台的图像/视频反馈日益普遍，能提供产品质量、缺陷、包装与真实使用场景的直观证据；但图像本身缺乏语境解释与主观观点，而撰写详细文字评论成本高，导致文本反馈有限。因此需要一种能依据图像自动生成评论草稿的辅助机制。

Method: 提出图像基座化的评价辅助任务，区别于一般图像描述，需商品级理解、情感估计与证据驱动的评论组织，并应对画质退化、过度放大、目标歧义、商品部分可见等现实挑战。框架由四个专职智能体构成：商品锚定、视觉情感估计、视觉证据生成、评论合成；并通过显式中间表示（商品实体、预测评分、证据摘要）提升可解释性与视觉落地性。

Result: 在Amazon Reviews Electronics数据集的精选子集上实验，表明能够从视觉反馈生成连贯、商品感知且情感感知的评论草稿。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个将图像基座化评价辅助形式化为多智能体视觉-语言推理问题的研究，为电商系统中的AI辅助评论撰写提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Visual feedback in the form of user-uploaded images and videos is becoming increasingly common in e-commerce platforms because it provides authentic evidence of product quality, defects, packaging conditions, and real-world usage. However, visual feedback alone often lacks the contextual explanations and subjective opinions necessary for informed decision-making, while many users provide limited textual feedback due to the effort required to compose detailed reviews. To bridge this gap, we introduce image-grounded review assistance, a novel task that aims to generate editable review drafts from user-uploaded product images. Unlike conventional image captioning, which focuses on objective visual description, the proposed task requires product-specific understanding, sentiment estimation, and evidence-driven review composition under challenging real-world conditions, including degraded image quality, excessive zoom-in, target ambiguity, and partial product visibility. We propose a multi-agent vision-language framework consisting of four specialised roles: product grounding, visual sentiment estimation, visual evidence generation, and review synthesis. The framework employs explicit intermediate representations, including product entities, predicted ratings, and evidence summaries, to improve interpretability and visual grounding. Experiments on a curated subset of the Amazon Reviews Electronics dataset demonstrate the feasibility of generating coherent, product-aware, and sentiment-aware review drafts from visual feedback. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study to formulate image-grounded review assistance as a multi-agent vision-language reasoning problem, providing a practical step toward AI-assisted review authoring in e-commerce systems.

</details>


### [63] [Multi-View Structure-from-Motion Enables Oriented Projective Shape Analysis in Three Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13263)
*Musab Alamoudi,Robert L. Paige,Vic Patrangenaru*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在方向投影形状（OPS）框架下，利用多视角SfM重建数据，首次实现三维OPS统计分析，对三立方体对象进行设计蓝图假设检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典投影形状（PS）模型基于单对未标定双目重建，仅能识别到允许反向的三维投影变换，因此只能使用"符号盲"的PS摘要。多视角SfM的捆绑调整仅识别到保向投影变换，从而移除了这一障碍，使得进行方向投影形状（OPS）分析成为可能。

Method: 采用Agisoft Metashape Professional 2.3.0构建n=8个SfM重建，通过复现原始数据的已发表双目构造来验证实现。在OPS框架下计算外在总方差指标，并对q=14个非框架地标进行蓝图假设的非参数检验，辅以样本量、照片数量和框架排序的稳健性分析。

Result: 在q=14个非框架地标上，蓝图假设均未被拒绝；SfM重建的集中度约为双目重建的26倍，重建精度显著提升。由于SfM数据高度集中，OPS指标渐近为PS指标的一半，这是集中性的结构性结果而非对象本身属性。样本量、照片数量和框架排序分析支持结论的稳健性。

Conclusion: 本文据所知其为首个三维OPS分析，验证了多视角SfM在方向投影形状统计推断中的可行性与精度优势；OPS指标渐近为PS指标一半属数据结构集中性所致，非对象固有属性。

Abstract: Projective shape analysis provides a geometric framework for studying landmark configurations in digital images acquired by pinhole cameras. In the classical projective shape (PS) model, three-dimensional configurations ($k$-ads) are represented as points in $(\mathrm{RP}^3)^q$, $q = k - 5$. A nonparametric test is developed in Patrangenaru et al. [12], for this framework, to determine whether an object matches a design blueprint, with each configuration reconstructed from a single uncalibrated stereo pair. Such two-view reconstructions are identified only up to a 3D projective transformation, which may reverse orientation, so that the sign-blind PS summary was the only available, before oriented projective shape (OPS) was recently considered. Multi-view Structure-from-Motion (SfM) technology removes this obstruction: its bundle adjustment is identified up to an orientation-preserving projective transformation. In this paper we revisit a well-cited three-cube object, from Patrangenaru et al. [12], with $n = 8$ SfM reconstructions built in Agisoft Metashape Professional 2.3.0, and validate our implementation by reproducing the published stereo construction from the original data. This allows us to perform what is to the best of our knowledge the first three-dimensional OPS analysis, compute its extrinsic total-variance index and perform statistical inference in this novel setting. Due to the high concentration of SfM data, the OPS index is asymptotically one-half the PS index, a structural consequence of concentration rather than a property of the object. Here our blueprint hypothesis is not rejected for any of the $q = 14$ non-frame landmarks, while the SfM reconstructions are about 26 times more concentrated than the stereo ones, a substantial gain in reconstruction precision. Sample-size, photograph-count, and frame-ordering analyses support the robustness of these conclusions.

</details>


### [64] [Semantic Privacy Protection with Utility Preservation for 3D Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13823)
*Jinchang zhang,Jiakai Lin,David Crandall,Guoyu Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于类别迁移的点云语义加密框架，在隐藏原始类别信息的同时保持任务可用性并支持授权恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云在采集、传输与跨机构共享中存在严重语义隐私风险；现有几何扰动或破坏性加密方法虽能降低原始类别可识别性，但往往损害下游可用性。

Method: 构建共享骨干Normalizing Flow的统一隐空间，结合LoRA与FiLM实现参数高效的类别条件适配；引入扩散引导的流对齐正则化隐分布；构建基于能量的类别转移图，通过全局匹配获得最优类别迁移表；用隐空间Neural ODE将源类隐变量演化为目标类隐变量，并经逆流解码为目标类点云。采用面向攻击者的指标NCRR、OCLR、OLRR评估隐私与效用。

Result: 在分类与分割基准上实现可控的语义变换，有效降低原始类别语义泄漏，同时保护域保持下游可学习性，并支持可靠的授权重建。

Conclusion: 该类别迁移式语义加密框架在点云隐私保护与任务效用之间取得平衡，兼顾防泄漏、下游可用性与授权恢复能力。

Abstract: Point cloud data face serious semantic privacy risks during acquisition, transmission, and cross-institutional sharing. Existing methods mostly rely on geometric perturbation or destructive encryption, which can reduce the recognizability of the original class but often impair downstream usability. This paper proposes a class-transfer-based semantic encryption framework for point clouds, aiming to conceal original class information while preserving task utility and supporting authorized recovery. Specifically, we construct a unified latent space with a shared-backbone Normalizing Flow, and combine LoRA and FiLM to achieve parameter-efficient class-conditional adaptation. We further introduce diffusion-guided flow alignment to regularize the latent distribution, construct an energy-based category transition graph, and obtain an optimal class-transfer table through global matching. Then, a latent-space Neural ODE continuously evolves source-class latents into target-class latents, which are decoded into target-class point clouds through the inverse flow. We adopt attacker-oriented metrics, including New-Class Recognition Rate (NCRR), Original-Class Leakage Rate (OCLR), and Original Label Recovery Rate (OLRR), to evaluate privacy and utility. Experiments on classification and segmentation benchmarks show that the proposed method achieves controllable semantic transformation, effectively reduces original-class semantic leakage, preserves downstream learnability in the protected domain, and supports reliable authorized reconstruction.

</details>


### [65] [BEACON: Behavior and Appearance Control for Subject-Specific Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13264)
*Pokrzywa Baptiste,Nabyl Quignon,Yara Bahram,Muhammad Osama Zeeshan,Antitza Dantcheva,Eric Granger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BEACON：一种轻量级人物特定视频生成框架，通过将视觉身份与表情行为解耦，以图像+参考视频双条件信号驱动生成，提升面部表情表现力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人物中心视频生成需同时保持视觉身份与人物特有的表情行为（面部随时间变化的情感表达动态）。然而主流方法仅以单张参考图像为条件，该图像不含时序动态信息，导致身份保持尚可但表情变化有限、主体特异性弱。

Method: 提出BEACON框架，将视觉身份与表情行为解耦，采用两个互补条件信号：编码身份的参考图像、捕捉主体特定面部动态的参考视频。基于预训练Wan视频扩散模型，仅微调约2000对数据、更新约1%参数。

Result: 在MEAD与RAVDESS数据集上，BEACON在面部表现力上优于当前最先进的视频生成方法，同时保持具有竞争力的身份保持效果，并支持身份-表情迁移。

Conclusion: 以双互补条件与极小参数高效微调实现人物特定、高表现力且身份一致的视频生成，验证了解耦身份与表情行为的有效性。

Abstract: Generating human-centric videos that preserve both visual identity and person-specific expressive behavior remains a fundamental challenge. In addition to reproducing appearance, a model must replicate the facial behaviors that characterize how a subject expresses emotion over time. However, most state-of-the-art methods condition generation on a single reference image, which contains no information about these temporal dynamics. As a result, they tend to preserve the subject's visual identity but often produce expressions with limited variation and weak subject specificity. To mitigate this issue, we introduce BEACON, a lightweight framework for person-specific video generation that produces more expressive videos by disentangling visual identity from expressive behavior. BEACON conditions generation on two complementary signals: a reference image encoding the identity and a reference video capturing subject-specific facial dynamics. By conditioning on these complementary signals, BEACON generates videos that better preserve both the subject's appearance and characteristic facial dynamics, while also supporting identity-expression transfer. Our experiments on the MEAD and RAVDESS datasets show that by fine-tuning on approximately 2,000 pairs and updating about 1% of the pretrained Wan video diffusion model, BEACON improves facial expressivity over state-of-the-art video generation methods while maintaining competitive identity preservation.

</details>


### [66] [Bi-Level Routing and Sparse Spatial Attention based Multi-View BEV 3D Object Detection for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14185)
*Jing Zhang,Jiaqi Liu,Zibo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Sparse-BEVNet，通过BRA、CGA和稀疏空间交叉注意力改进BEV多视图3D检测，在nuScenes上mAP达45.2%、NDS达54.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BEV多视图3D目标检测面临计算复杂度高、多尺度特征提取不足以及密集2D到BEV视角转换效率低三大挑战。

Method: 一是在图像特征提取网络中引入双层路由注意力机制（BRA）以降低主干计算负担；二是在特征融合模块采用级联组注意力（CGA），在不增加额外计算开销的情况下增强不同层级特征的深度交互；三是用稀疏空间交叉注意力机制替代传统密集视角投影流程。

Result: 在公开nuScenes数据集上，mAP为45.2%，NDS为54.5%，相较基线模型分别提升3.6%和2.8%。

Conclusion: Sparse-BEVNet可有效缓解BEV检测的计算复杂度与视角转换效率问题，并在精度指标上优于基线。

Abstract: Bird's Eye View (BEV)-based multi-view 3D object detection suffers from challenges of computational complexity, multi-scale feature extraction, and efficiency of dense 2D-to-BEV view transformation. To address these problems, this paper proposes an improved BEV 3D object detection algorithm Sparse-BEVNet. Firstly, a Bi-Level Routing Attention (BRA) mechanism is introduced into the image feature extraction network to reduce the computational burden of the backbone. Second, Cascaded Group Attention (CGA) is employed in the feature fusion module, which enhances deep interaction across features of different hierarchical levels without introducing additional computational overhead. Furthermore, a Sparse Spatial Cross-Attention mechanism is adopted to replace the conventional dense view projection pipeline. Experimental results on the public nuScenes dataset demonstrate that the proposed method achieves a mean Average Precision (mAP) of 45.2% and a nuScenes Detection Score (NDS) of 54.5%, corresponding to 3.6% and 2.8% improvements relative to the baseline model, respectively.

</details>


### [67] [Does Attention-Guided Masking Really Help Object Discovery in Object-Centric Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15187)
*Youliang Tao,Yanhua Han,Bin Zhao,Juho Kannala,Joni Pajarinen,Rongzhen Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出注意力引导掩码（AGM）以改进OCL自监督，但实验表明其不总是优于随机掩码（RM），提示利用内部注意力语义改进掩码解码存在风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Slot Attention聚合特征时已具备目标发现能力，作者希望利用该注意力设计更好的图像块掩码策略，以提供更优的自监督信号。

Method: 提出Attention Guided Masking（AGM），利用Slot Attention中注意力来引导图像块掩码，替代DIAS中的均匀随机掩码（RM），并在六个数据集上进行对比评估。

Result: AGM并非总优于RM：在无条件槽初始化下，AGM在真实纹理数据集（COCO、VOC）上显著改善背景分割；但无论条件还是无条件初始化、跨数据集，前景目标发现持平或下降。

Conclusion: 不建议OCL研究者尝试利用内部注意力语义来改进掩码解码式的OCL方法，该方向风险较高。

Abstract: Object-Centric Learning (OCL) aims to decompose images into objects without human annotations. A major family of mainstream methods uses Slot Attention to aggregate image features into object-level representations and then from them reconstructs masked image content, i.e., Random Masking (RM), to provide self-supervision. The recent method DIAS simply masks image patches at uniform randomness yet achieves competitive object discovery accuracy. Since attention during aggregation already possesses object discovery ability, we explore using it to develop a better image patch masking strategy, i.e., Attention Guided Masking (AGM), thereby providing better self-supervision. Results on six recognized datasets show that AGM does not always outperform RM. Under unconditional slot initialization, AGM substantially improves background segmentation on datasets with realistic textures (COCO and VOC); Regardless of conditional or unconditional slot initialization and across datasets, foreground object discovery remains comparable or decreases. We suggest peer researchers in the OCL community that attempts to exploit internal attention semantics to improve OCL with masked decoding are risky. Our source code, model checkpoints and evaluation logs will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [68] [Don't Send What You Don't Need: Question-Guided Token Pruning as a Privacy Defense for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15671)
*Md Khalid Syfullah,Alvi Ataur Khalil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QPriv-VL，一种问题引导、隐私感知的视觉token剪枝框架，适用于FL/SL/USL，在传输前剪除低效用或高敏感视觉token，在多个VQA数据集和四类隐私攻击下同时提升隐私保护与传输效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQA与VLM在隐私敏感、带宽受限场景中应用增多；FL、SL、USL虽保持原始数据本地化，但传输全部视觉token成本高且可能泄露隐私信息，因此需要在传输前对视觉token进行效用与隐私双重的筛选。

Method: 核心为轻量级动态阈值预测器（DTP），单次前向即可联合估计样本级剪枝比例与token级保留掩码；DTP将视觉patch与池化问题嵌入的跨模态相似度得到的问题相关性，与基于冻结DINOv2特征导出的敏感度信号相结合，从而在无需敏感度标签的情况下抑制潜在敏感区域并保留对答题有用的patch。

Result: 在GQA、OK-VQA、VQAv2、SLAKE、VQA-RAD、PathVQA上针对FSHA、FORA、iDLG及属性推断型成员推断攻击进行评估：DTP以显著更少的传输token达到或超过固定比例剪枝；VQA-RAD上成员推断攻击成功率由0.99降至0.76-0.79，FSHA/FORA重建PSNR低于固定比例剪枝，并以约40%的原始视觉token预算保持有竞争力的VQA精度；敏感度排除比为1.20±0.18，表明优先移除隐私敏感patch；可解释性分析显示保留策略随问题语义调整而非依赖通用视觉显著性。

Conclusion: QPriv-VL验证了以问题引导的动态token剪枝可在联邦/拆分学习场景中同时实现隐私保护与通信压缩，且保留决策具有语义自适应性。

Abstract: Visual Question Answering (VQA) with Vision-Language Models (VLMs) is increasingly used in privacy-sensitive and bandwidth-constrained settings. Federated Learning (FL), Split Learning (SL), and U-Shaped Split Learning (USL) keep raw data local, but transmitting all visual tokens across a model partition remains costly and can expose private information. We propose QPriv-VL, a question-guided, privacy-aware token-pruning framework for FL, SL, and USL that prunes visual tokens before transmission based on task utility and privacy sensitivity. Its core component is a lightweight Dynamic Threshold Predictor (DTP) that jointly estimates a sample-specific pruning ratio and a token-level retention mask in one forward pass. DTP combines question relevance, computed from cross-modal similarity between visual patches and the pooled question embedding, with a sensitivity signal derived from frozen DINOv2 features. This allows the model to suppress potentially sensitive regions while preserving patches useful for answering the question, without requiring sensitivity labels. We evaluate QPriv-VL on GQA, OK-VQA, VQAv2, SLAKE, VQA-RAD, and PathVQA against four privacy attack families: FSHA, FORA, iDLG, and attribute-inference membership inference attacks. DTP matches or outperforms fixed-ratio pruning while using substantially fewer transmitted tokens. On VQA-RAD, it reduces membership-inference attack success from 0.99 to 0.76-0.79, lowers FSHA and FORA reconstruction PSNR relative to fixed-ratio pruning, and preserves competitive VQA accuracy using about 40% of the original visual-token budget. A sensitivity exclusion ratio of 1.20 +/- 0.18 indicates preferential removal of privacy-sensitive patches, while explainability analysis shows that retention adapts to question semantics rather than generic visual saliency.

</details>
